Preparation:

    1. Service and check the catwalk, drill floor and moonpool air hoists including
      the man riding winches and check the wires are in good condition.
    2. Service the riser handling and riser test tools.
    3. Grease the riser spider and check the gimbal hydraulic system.
    4. Have sufficient air built up in the air accumulator bottles.
    5. Check/service all grub screws on the riser protectors.
    6. Clear the rig floor of any equipment not needed for riser handling.
    7. Check the idler sheave walkways and be sure there  is no staging or
       scaffolding built around them.
    8.  Pull back the riser tensioner wires to give clearance for the BOP cranes.
    9. The termination joint, pup joints, crossover joint, riser  fill up joint, landing
         joint, and telescopic joint are stored on the forward pipe deck.
   10. The slick joints and buoyant riser are stored in the aft deck riser bays.
   11. Lay out the mousehole.
   12. Clean and oil or grease the BOP and LMRP skidding beams.
   13. Connect the transverse BOP test stump skid hydraulics and test the same.

i) Run the BOP:

    1. Move the Drill ship off the well head (+ or -100 meters).
    2. Lay down the mousehole.
    3. Inform the DPO and Engine Control Room that the BOP is about to be
      skidded to the well centre.
    4. Extend the spider beams to the skidding position and lock in place.
    5. Attach the BOP crane to the BOP and take up the slack.
    6. Skid the BOP to well centre following with the BOP crane. Unlock the
      wellhead connector from the skid test stump and retract the ring gasket
       retainers.
    7. Pick up the BOP, install supports as required, remove the old ring gasket
      from the test stump, grease the connector and clean the ring grooves.
    8.  Install a new VX ring gasket on the test stump, remove the supports and
      land the BOP. Extend the ring gasket retainers and lift the BOP clear of the
       skid.
    9. Remove the skid to the BOP storage bay.
   10. Retract the skid beam lock pins and move the skid beam to the BOP
      landing position, and then engage the lock pins.
   11. Lower the BOP until the top beam is just above the spider beam support
      dogs. Extend the BOP support dogs and land the BOP. Remove the BOP
      crane.
   12. Clean the pod receptacles, choke and  kill stabs and the riser connector
      mandrel. Cover the mandrel.
   13. Inform the DPO and Engine Control Room that the LMRP is about to be
     moved to well centre.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                41


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 42 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                      January 2011
                     Revision No.
                                        Manual Nr:
                           0
                     Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                       Document Title:
                                                                 Procedures Manual
                    Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by

14. Skid the LMRP to the forward side of the moonpool, unlock the riser
   connector and retract the ring gasket retainers. Lift the LMRP off the test
   stump skid, install supports and move the skid with the ring gasket on it to
   the LMRP storage bay.
15. Grease the connector, clean the ring groove, remove the cover from the
   mandrel and install a new AX ring gasket.
16. Move the LMRP with the BOP crane to WC, and then lower the LMRP onto
   the BOP. Extend the ring gasket retainers and lock the riser and kill and
   choke connectors. Take a test pull. Remove the BOP crane.
17. Pick up a stand of drill pipe in the elevators and run  it down through the
  BOP ensuring the tool joints are clear of the rams.
18. Perform a complete BOP function test on both pods. Pull the stand out of
   the BOP and function test the shear rams. Record the time taken and fluid
   volume for each function.
19. Perform an EDS function test.
20. Rack back the stand then change to the 500ton bails and elevators. Do not
   connect the link tilt cylinders.
21. Unlock the top drive pipe handler.
22. Remove the rotary table insert bushings, master bushings and adaptor
    ring. Take measures to prevent personnel from falling through the open
    rotary.
23. Install the gimbal, riser spider and control console in the rotary.
24. Pick up the termination joint from the forward pipe deck with the crane.
   Place  it on the drill floor with the box end towards well centre, check the
   stabs and seals, change as required, oil/grease the stabs as required and
   set it in the spider. Remove the riser handling tool if used
25. Put the first joint of riser onto the riser trolley and pick it up as per section
      (iii) of this procedure “Run Riser Joints from the Riser Bays” , tailing  it in
   with the tailing arm
26. Make up the running tool bolts (Cameron RD 250-300ft/lbs).
27. Orientate the riser joint and stab it onto the termination joint in the rotary.
   Slack off the full weight of the riser joint to ensure proper engagement with
   the previous joint. Make up the bolts (Cameron RD 250-300ft/lbs).
28. Check the riser adaptor box and the  kill and choke stabs are clean.
   Change any damaged seals and oil/grease as required
29. Run in and stab the termination joint and first joint onto the riser adaptor.
  Make up the bolts the coupling bolts (Cameron RD 250-300ft/lbs).
30. Take a test lift and re-torque the coupling bolts.
31. Pick the BOP up clear of the BOP support dogs and retract them.
32. Unlock and retract the spider beams.
33. Record the flex joint, LMRP and BOP slope indicator readings.
34. Run the BOP in the water with  all  kill & choke  fail safes and isolation
   valves in the open position.
35. Record the weight of the BOP assembly, termination joint and first slick
    joint.


                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                42


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 43 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   36. Close the riser spider and slowly land the riser in the spider and back out
      the riser handling tool bolts, and then remove the riser handling tool.
   37. Close the kill and choke isolation valves and fill the choke and kill lines with
       water.
   38. Install the riser testing tool and make up the bolts (Cameron RD 250-
        300ft/lbs). Pressure test the kill and choke lines to 250psi and 10000psi for
     5 and 10 minutes respectively (as per API recommendations) and the
      booster line to 3000psi.

ii) Run Riser Joints from the Forward Pipe Deck:

    1.  Lift the riser joint from the pipe rack onto the catwalk with the starboard
      crane using a two leg spreader and tag lines.
    2. Shackle the crane and the catwalk air winch to the forward end and a drill
        floor air winch to the aft end.
    3. Ensure good communication between the crane operator and air winch
       driver with the deck foreman in clear view.
    4. Pick up the joint with the crane and the drill floor air winch sliding it up the
    V door ramp and keeping it slightly below horizontal, controlling it with the
      catwalk air winch.
    5. Land the flange on the drill floor and remove the protector as required.
     Check the riser, kill, choke and booster line stabs. Change any damaged
      seals and oil/grease as required.
    6.  Install the riser handling tool and make up the bolts (Cameron RD 250-
       300ft/lbs, Vetco MR6E 900-1000ft/lbs).
    7. Rotate the elevators to face aft and lift them with an air winch, at the same
      time lowering the blocks to swing the elevators out over the riser handling
        tool.
    8. Lower the elevators onto the riser handling tool and latch them securing
      the latch with rope.
    9. Remove the drill floor air winches from the riser joint and the elevators.
   10. Pick up the joint with the elevators slacking off on the catwalk winch,
       adjusting the height with the crane to bring the box end into the drill floor.
      Extend the riser tailing arm and continue picking up until the joint rests
       against the arm.
   11. Remove the crane and the catwalk air winch.
   12. Check the riser pins and boxes and the kill, choke and booster line stabs.
     Change any damaged seals and oil/grease the stabs as required.
   13. Swing the joint into well centre with the tailing arm, orientate the kill, choke
     and booster line stabs as required and stab the joint.
   14. Retract the tailing arm.
   15. Slack off the full weight of the riser joint to ensure proper engagement.
     Make up  the  bolts (Cameron RD  250-300ft/lbs, Vetco MR6E  900-
       1000ft/lbs).
   16. Take a test  lift, re-torque the bolts and then take the weight, open the
       spider and inform the moonpool the string is ready to run in.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                43


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 44 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   17. Lower the string slowly and stop to install the MUX clamps as required.
   18. When running the riser fill up joint install the control hose and function test
      the valve. Secure the control hose to the riser with clamps as required

iii) Run Riser Joints from the Riser Bays:

    1. Open the catwalk lifting bridge.
    2. Move the riser gantry crane over the first riser joint, open the hooks and
      lower the beam onto the riser joint. Close the hooks onto the riser.
    3.  Lift the joint and move it over the riser trolley.
    4. Land the riser on the forward and aft trolleys with the joint overhanging the
      forward trolley by 1.5m.
    5. Open the riser gantry crane hooks and move the gantry crane to the next
        joint of riser.
    6. Lower the catwalk lifting bridge.
    7. Check the riser pins and boxes and the kill, choke and booster line stabs.
     Change any damaged seals and oil/grease the stabs as required.
    8. Flush lines with water to remove any build up of debris (dust, rust, dry
      mud).
    9. Extend the riser tailing arm past the riser spider.
   10. Move the riser trolleys forward towards well centre.
   11. Attach the aft drill floor air winch to the riser handling tool and install it on
      the riser joint on the riser trolley using snatch blocks on king posts to pull
      the handling tool into position as required.
   12. Make up  the  bolts (Cameron RD  250-300ft/lbs, Vetco MR6E  900-
       1000ft/lbs).
   13. Rotate the elevators to face forward and lift them with an air winch at the
     same time lowering the blocks to swing the elevators out over the riser
      handling tool.
   14. Lower the elevators onto the riser handling tool and latch them securing
      the latch with rope.
   15. Pick up the joint with the elevators at a steady speed slowing down as the
        aft trolley comes to the end of the track. Retract the tailing arm against the
        riser and continue picking up the joint, holding it back with the tailing arm.
   16. Remove the protector from the joint in the rotary as required and check the
        riser pins and boxes and the  kill, choke and booster line stabs. Change
      any seals as required and oil/grease the stabs as required.
   17. Rotate the riser to align the kill, choke and booster line stabs and stab the
        joint onto the joint in the rotary table slacking off the full weight of the riser
        joint to ensure proper engagement.
   18. Extend the tailing arm to its full extent beyond the rotary.
   19. Make up the coupling bolts (Cameron RD 250-300ft/lbs, Vetco MR6E 900-
       1000ft/lbs).
   20. Take a test  lift, re-torque the bolts and then take the weight, open the
       spider and inform the moonpool the string is ready to run in.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                44


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 45 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   21. Lower the riser string until the joint is almost down then close the riser
       spider. Record riser ID number and the string weight before landing in the
       spider.
   22. Slowly release the riser weight into the riser spider.
   23. Back out the bolts and lift the riser handling tool off the riser joint.
   24. Install a protector on the pin in the rotary if required.
   25. Fill the kill and choke lines with water while picking up next joint.

iv) Pick Up the Telescopic Joint:

Ensure good communication between drill floor, pipe deck, crane operator, spider
deck area and pod reels (Radios).

The telescopic joint will normally be stored on the starboard side of the forward
pipe deck.

    1. Jump the ROV and check the TV monitor on the rig floor is receiving a
     good picture. Position the ROV and carry out a riser, BOP, slope indicator,
     and wellhead inspection. This inspection should be witnessed by the SSE
      (Sub Sea Engineer).
    2.  Lift the telescopic joint directly from the pipe rack to the drill floor with the
      main block of the starboard crane. Rig the  lift with a two leg spreader.
        Install the catwalk air winch on the forward end and a drill floor air winch
     on the aft end.
    3. Ensure good communication between the crane operator and air winch
       drivers with the deck foreman in clear view.
    4.  Lift the telescopic joint to the drill floor with the crane sliding  it up the V
      door ramp as far as possible, controlling it with the drill floor and catwalk
        air winches.
    5. Land the packer box on the drill floor and remove the pin end protector.
    6. Using a drill floor air winch, install a riser handling tool onto the slip joint
     and make up the bolts (Vetco MR6E 900-1000ft/lbs).
    7. Rotate the elevators to face aft and  lift them with an air winch while
      lowering  travelling blocks to swing the elevators out above the  riser
      handling tool.
    8. Lower the elevators onto the riser handling tool and latch them. Secure
      the latch with rope.
    9. Remove the drill floor air winches from the elevators and telescopic joint.
   10. Ensure good communication between the driller, crane operator and air
      winch driver with the deck foreman in clear view.
   11. Lift the telescopic joint with the elevators, lowering the deck crane at the
     same time,  until the box end of the telescopic joint  is resting on the
      catwalk with the outer barrel resting against the top of the V door.
   12. Remove the top sling and spreader. Reposition the crane to the box
     end of the telescopic joint and pick  it up restraining  it with the
      catwalk air winch.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                45


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 46 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   13. Flush through the kill, choke and booster lines to confirm they are clear.
     Check  all  the gooseneck  stabs. Change any damaged  seals and
       oil/grease as required.
   14. Continue picking up the telescopic  joint with the elevators and crane
       allowing the box end to swing in towards the top of the V door ramp with
      the catwalk air winch and adjusting the height with the crane.
   15. Extend the riser tailing arm to its full extent.
   16. When inside the V door hold the box end back with the tailing arm and
     remove the crane and catwalk air winch.
   17. Check the seals in the riser and kill and choke line boxes.
   18. Continue to hoist the telescopic joint with the elevators, and swing it in to
      the rotary with the tailing arm. Remove the protector from the riser joint in
      the rotary.
   19. Orientate the  kill and choke lines and stab the box onto the pin in the
       rotary.
   20. Retract the tailing arm.
   21. Slack  off  the  full  weight  of  the  telescopic  joint  to  ensure  proper
     engagement with the previous joint. Make up the coupling bolts (Vetco
    MR6E 900-1000ft/lbs).
   22. Take a test  lift, re-torque the bolts and then take the weight, open the
       spider.
   23. Inform the moonpool etc the string is ready to run.

v) Control of the MUX, Conduit and Riser Fill Valve Reels:

    1. The MUX cables are very fragile and must be treated with great care.
    2. While the riser is pulled the MUX cables must be released from the riser.
    3.  Install walkways across the spider beams with  fall arrestors above for
      access to the riser kill and choke lines to remove the clamps.
    4. The MUX, conduit and riser fill valve hose reel controls are operated by the
      Sub-sea engineer or Electronics Technician.
    5. The hands free talk back system is to be tested before the operation starts.
    6. The cellar deck camera is to be focussed on the control reels.
    7. Ensure the manual brakes are released and that the reels are free.
    8. A warning must be given by the driller prior to moving the riser string.
    9. Always keep slack in the MUX lines.

vi) Kill, Choke and Booster Line Tests:

    1. A permit to work must be in force prior to commencing high pressure
       testing.
    2. The kill, choke and booster lines are normally tested every 10 joints. The
         first test must be done once the termination joint and the first riser joint are
       run.
    3. Land the riser on the spider and remove the riser running tool (if required).
    4.  Fill up the lines with water until free of air.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                46


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 47 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

    5.  Install the riser testing tool.
    6. Connect the  testing manifold  to the  testing  unit which must have a
      pressure recorder suitable for high and low pressure tests.
    7. Before connecting the testing manifold to the test plugs flush through the
       lines then make up the connections.
    8. Check the line up and pressure test the line up to testing manifold.
    9. Pressure test the kill and choke lines to 250 psi for 5 mins and 10000psi
        for 10 min and the booster line to 3000psi.
   10. All tests performed against the kill & choke isolation valves and the booster
        line valves.
   11. Bleed off the pressure and remove the riser test tool.

vii) Land the BOP:

    1.  Notify the moonpool and the ROV personnel before lowering the telescopic
        joint to land the inner barrel mandrel on the spider.
    2. Back out the bolts and remove the riser handling tool.
    3. Pick up the landing joint from the forward pipe deck as per section (iii) of
        this procedure, “Run Riser Joints from the Forward Pipe Deck”, and install
            it on the slip joint.
    4. Make up the coupling bolts (Vetco MR6E 900-1000ft/lbs). Take a test  lift
     and re-torque the bolts.
    5. Pick up the riser string, open the riser spider and notify the moonpool and
      the ROV personnel before lowering the slip joint.
    6. Lock the load ring onto the telescopic joint with the rotating dogs (6).
       Visually confirm  all  six dogs are  locked. The SSE  will witness  this
       operation.
    7. Once the load ring is locked to slip joint, take weight of the load ring (20klb
      over pull) and remove the safety slings from the load ring. Unlock the load
       ring support dogs.
    8. With ROV observing, lower the telescopic  joint to a working level for
        installing the kill, choke and booster line goosenecks and the hydraulics to
        slip joint.
    9. The last MUX clamps should be installed on the telescopic joint so that
      they will be just below the splash zone when BOPs are landed. (Two
      clamps on each side). Install support saddles for the MUX and hot line
      hose on the goosenecks.
   10. Using the drill floor and cellar deck air winches lower the kill line, booster
        line and choke line goosenecks (in that order) from the forward port side of
      the drill floor and stab them into the receptacles on the telescopic joint,
        install the lock blocks and retainers.
   11. The  kill, choke and booster lines can be pressure tested at this point to
      ensure the gooseneck seals are good before landing the BOP.
   12. The  riser tensioners must be pressured up well  in advance (24 hrs
      minimum).


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                47


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 48 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   13. Adjust the riser tensioner and CMC pressures as required for landing the
    BOP (2/3 on riser and 1/3 on CMC).
   14. Spool off sufficient MUX cable and conduit hoses from the reels to allow
        for landing and heave considerations.
   15. With the BOP 5-10 meters above well head depth move the ship to
       position the BOP above the wellhead.
   16. Position the ROV to observe the wellhead and the guide funnel. Confirm
      the wellhead connector is open, and the ring gasket is in place.
   17. Move the ship into position and land the BOP with 50klb down weight.
   18. Re-position the ROV to observe the wellhead connector position indicator.
      Lock the connecter confirming visually with the ROV.
   19. Perform a pick up test with100klb over-pull.
   20. Adjust the riser tensioners as required.
   21. Record the slope indicator readings with the ROV. Record the ship and
    ROV headings.
   22. Close the shear rams and pressure test the VX gasket to 500 psi.
   23. Set neutral weight at the telescopic joint lock dogs.
   24. Unlock the telescopic joint hydraulic latch and use the CMC to pull back
      the landing joint and stroke out the inner barrel. Land the crossover in the
       spider.

viii) Install the Diverter:

    1. Back out the coupling bolts and tail out the landing joint using the tailing
      arm. Install a protector on the pin in the rotary.
    2. Lay down the landing joint to the forward catwalk, as per section (iv) of
      procedure 4.1.8 - Pulling the BOP, “Pull Riser Joints to the Forward Pipe
      Deck”.
    3. Pick up the diverter from the forward pipe deck with the crane using slings
       of the correct length to keep the diverter horizontal. Care to be taken not to
      crush the flow line seal hydraulic piping. Use the catwalk and  drill floor
      winches to control the load.
    4. Land the diverter as far as possible into the drill floor with the handling tool
      towards well centre.
    5. Rotate the elevators to face aft and lift them with an air winch at the same
      time lowering the blocks to swing the elevators out over the riser diverter
      handling tool.
    6. Lower the elevators onto the handling tool and latch them securing the
       latch with rope.
    7. Holding the diverter back with the catwalk winch  lift  it with the elevators
     and crane initially and then lower the crane and catwalk winch to swing the
       diverter in towards the extended tailing arm.
    8. Remove the crane and catwalk air winch.
    9. Remove the protector from the joint in the rotary.
   10. Stab the diverter onto the telescopic joint inner barrel mandrel and make
     up the bolts (Vetco MR6E 900-1000ft/lbs). Retract the tailing arm.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                48


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 49 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   11. Remove the ball joint clamp.
   12. Pick up the diverter and inner barrel and open the riser spider.
   13. Orientate, run in and land the diverter in the diverter housing.
   14. Install the hydraulic manifold.
   15. The hydraulic supply at the diverter manifold should read 2800 - 3000 psi.
               •  Diverter element pressure should read 0 psi.
               •   Slip joint pressure should read 0 psi.
               •  The control valves should be as follows:
               •  Diverter element = Open
               •  Diverter Lockdown Dogs = Unlock
               •  Insert Packer Lockdown Dogs = Block (centre)
               •  Set manifold pressure to 1500 psi.
               •  Put diverter lock down dogs to "lock" and verify the 6 dogs are
                 extended.
   16. Take a 20klb over pull
   17. Set the diverter element to 750 psi then change this function to "remote".
   18. To remove the diverter handling tool turn the mandrel 21¾ turns to the left.
     Remove the diverter handling tool. Take measures to prevent anyone
        falling through the rotary.
   19. Flush all lines with sea water (minimum one internal volume).
   20. Fill the riser with sea water and energize the telescopic joint upper packer
       with sufficient pressure to make a seal (typically 50 to 100 psi) and the flow
        line seals to 200psi.
   21. Check the telescopic joint packer and flow line seals are holding and there
      are no leaks on the hydraulic manifold.
   22. Rig down the riser spider and gimbal.
   23. Install the adaptor ring, master bushings, insert bushings and hole cover.
   24. Change to the 5” automatic elevators and 350t bails. Connect the link  tilt
       cylinders.
   25. Pick up and install the mouse hole.
   26. RIH the BOP test tool and install the diverter packer. Pressure test the
     BOP, as per procedure 4.2.7.3 – Pressure Test the BOP Subsea, and
      pressure test the riser and diverter to 500psi as per procedure 4.2.7.4 –
      Pressure Test the Diverter system.

Post Task Actions:

    1. Sub Sea engineer to ensure the MUX, conduit and riser fill valve reels are
       locked.
    2. Sub Sea Engineer to monitor fluid used by BOP.
    3. Secure the Riser and BOP gantry cranes and riser trolleys.
    4. Sign off all permits.
    5.  After the BOP has been run, remove all handling gear and return it to its
      storage area.

4.1.8  Pulling the BOP

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to ensure smooth and safe operation when
pulling the BOP stack on Riser.

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required activities, which need to be
carried out prior and during the pulling of the BOP stack on Riser.

Responsible Persons:

Rig Superintendent / Toolpusher / Subsea Engineer / Driller / Crane Operator /
Deck Foreman

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

    1. Hold a Toolbox meeting. Review the procedure and JSA (Job Safety
       Analysis).
    2. Acquire weather forecasts and confirm a suitable weather window  is
       available for the operation.
    3. The BOP stack should not be pulled if rig motion is excessive (pitch, roll &
      heave) Crane limitations are a maximum of 40 knots wind speed and
     maximum of 1.5 degrees pitch and roll for handling the slip joint and riser.
    4. The rig is to be moved 100m off location as soon as possible after the BOP
      has been unlatched and pulled clear of the guide funnel.
    5. Permit to work fully approved and signed with recommendations.
    6. The clear-call system between the rig floor and the moonpool is to be
       operational before the job begins.
    7. Work vests (correctly donned and secured) must be worn when working in
       or near the side of the moonpool.
    8. PPE worn at all times.
    9. Safety harnesses attached to fall arrestors must be worn when working
      from the spider beams or from heights.
   10. Pin protectors must be correctly secured before any riser is removed from
      the catwalk.
   11. Never run an air winch down through the rotary to the moonpool for man
        riding.
   12. All air hose connections must be secured with "R" clips and whipchecks.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                50


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 51 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011

                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and

   13. Lifting gear fully inspected (check for appropriate colour coding, damage,
       malfunction, etc).
   14. All non essential personnel to keep clear of drill floor, raised catwalk and
        riser storage areas.
   15. Stand clear in the moon pool when the riser is moving through the rotary.
   16. Ensure good communication between driller, MUX reel operator and the
      crew working in moon pool.
   17. The BOP stack should not be pulled if vessel motion is outside operating
        limits for BOP handling, or weather window is not sufficient to run and land
      the BOPs. Crane limitations are a maximum of 40 knots wind speed and
     maximum of 1.5 degrees (full amplitude) pitch and roll for handling the slip
         joint, landing joint etc.
   18. The riser tensioners are numbered 1 to 10 clockwise from Port Forward.

Equipment Required:

    1. 500 ton bails and elevators
    2. Riser gantry crane
    3. Riser spider & operating remote panel
    4. Riser gimbal
    5. Riser trolley forward and aft. Run test the aft trolley
    6. Riser handling tools (Cameron RD and Vetco MR6E) on the drill floor
    7. BOP gantry crane
    8.  Drill floor tailing arm tested
    9. Spider beams
   10. Skidding system
   11. Diverter handling tool
   12. Load ring handling tool to remove the load ring from the diverter housing
   13. Riser tally to be available for Tool Pusher, Driller and Crane operator
   14. Fall arrestors and safety harnesses
   15. Rags
   16. Pipe wrenches, crescent wrenches
   17. Shackles with spare retaining rings/pins and spare slings
   18. Allen wrenches
   19. Pneumatic impact wrenches  with pressure  regulators  set below  the
       required torque of the riser bolts
   20. Torque wrenches set for the required torque of the riser bolts (Cameron
    RD 250-300ft/lbs, Vetco MR6E 900-1000ft/lbs)
   21. Vetco MR6E dog engagement go/no go tool
   22. Tools (2) to remove the MUX, conduit and riser fill valve hose clamps.
   23. Boat hook
   24. Set of tools, bars, spanners, hammers, brushes and rags
   25. Run test the riser trolley

Preparation:


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                51


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 52 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                      January 2011
                     Revision No.
                                        Manual Nr:
                           0
                     Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                       Document Title:
                                                                 Procedures Manual
                    Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by

1. Have the catwalk and drill floor air winches serviced and checked by the
   mechanic and check the wires are in good condition.
2. Service the riser handling tools.
3. Grease the riser spider and check the gimbal hydraulic system.
4. Have sufficient air built up in the air accumulator bottles.
5. Check/service all grub screws on the riser protectors.
6. Jump the ROV and check the TV monitor on the rig floor is receiving a
   good  picture.  Position   it  to observe the wellhead connector  position
    indicator (give sufficient notice for them to reach the seabed).
7. Clear the rig floor of any equipment not needed for riser handling.
8. Service all moonpool air hoists including the manriding winches.
9. Check the idler sheave walkways and be sure there  is no staging or
   scaffolding built around them.
10. Displace the BOP, riser boost and kill and choke lines to seawater.
11. Thoroughly flush the kill and choke lines, BOP and riser.
12. Vent off the telescopic joint packer and run booster pump to flush the
   telescopic joint locking receptacle.
13. Leave the kill and choke fail safes and the booster line valves open. Jet
   inside the BOP to remove any accumulations of mud or cement to prevent
   problems with testing the BOP on the test stump.
14. Clear any extraneous equipment from the riser bays
15. The slings for the diverter and telescopic joint on the drill floor ready for
   use.
16. The termination joint, pup joints, crossover joint, riser  fill up joint, landing
    joint, and telescopic joint are stored on the forward pipe deck.
17. The slick joints and buoyant riser are stored in the aft deck riser bays.
18. Pull the catwalk air winch up to the drill floor.
19. Lay out the mousehole.





                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                52


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 53 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011

Personnel Requirements:


           Position           Number          Location
Rig Superintendent/Toolpusher        1       Supervision
Driller                            1     DCR
Assistant Driller                    1       Control reels
Derrickman                       1          Drill floor-Riser trolley
Floormen                         2          Drill floor-Raised catwalk
Floormen                         2        Cellar deck
Roustabouts                      2       Riser bays
Crane Operator                    1       Riser gantry crane
Sub-Sea Engineer                  1       Control reels

i) Lay Out the Diverter:

    1. Vent off the telescopic joint packer.
    2. Vent off the diverter flow line seals and diverter packer lock down dogs.
    3. Remove the hydraulic manifold from the diverter and install the protection
       plate. Cover the hydraulic manifold.
    4. Unlock the top drive pipe handler.
    5. Change out the 350ton elevators and bails to the 500t elevators and bails.
     Do not install the link tilt pins.
    6. Remove the rotary table insert bushings, master bushings and adaptor
        ring. Take measures to prevent personnel from falling through the open
       rotary.
    7.  Install the diverter handling tool by turning the mandrel 21¾ turns to the
        right.
    8.  Install the gimbal, riser spider and console in the rotary. Function test the
       spider.
    9. Latch the elevators onto the diverter handling tool with the elevators facing
         aft.
   10. Take a test pull and slack off again. Unlock the diverter lock down dogs (6)
     and check they are retracted. Vent off the diverter pressure.
   11. Pull the diverter cleaning the flow line area of cuttings and mud build up as
            it is being raised.
   12. Set the telescopic joint inner barrel mandrel in the riser spider.
   13. Install the clamp on the ball  joint. Back out the bolts with the impact
      wrenches ensuring the dogs are fully disengaged.
   14. Lift the diverter sufficiently to ensure the joint is broken.  It may require
     some movement of the elevators to break the joint due to the low weight of
      the telescopic joint inner barrel.
   15. Pick up the diverter and  tail  it out towards the forward v door with the
        tailing arm. Install a protector on the pin in the rotary.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                53


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 54 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   16. Attach the forward starboard crane to the ball joint housing with a sling.
   17. Ensure good communication between the driller and crane operator with
      the deck foreman in clear view of both.
   18. Take the weight with the crane, retract the tailing arm, then lower the
       elevators and crane to lay the diverter down on the drill floor.
   19. Attach an air winch to the elevators, lift and unlatch them lifting them clear
       of the diverter handling tool.
   20. Pick up the blocks slacking off on the air winch when the elevators are
       clear of the joint in the rotary.
   21. Attach the catwalk air winch to the bottom of the diverter and a drill floor air
      winch to the top of the handling tool. Attach the crane with a two leg
      spreader to two slings on the body of the diverter (ensure the flow line seal
       hydraulic piping is not damaged by the slings).
   22. Ensure good communication between the crane operator and air winch
       drivers with the deck foreman in clear view.
   23. Pick up the diverter with the crane and controlling  it with the two  air
      winches, lower it to the catwalk.
   24. Remove the two air winches and install tag lines then  lift the diverter into
         its storage bay.
   25. Check with DP that the ship is on the correct heading for the BOP.

ii) Unlatch the BOP:

    1. Pick up the landing joint from the forward pipe deck as per section (iii) of
      procedure 4.1.7 - Running the BOP, “Run Riser Joints from the Forward
      Pipe Deck”.
    2.  Install the  riser handling  tool make up the  bolts (Vetco MR6E 900-
       1000ft/lbs). Ensure the dogs are fully engaged with the go/no go tool.
    3. Remove the protector from the joint in the rotary. Clean the pin and box
     and oil/grease as required.
    4. Stab the landing joint slacking off the full weight of the joint to ensure the
      connection is fully engaged.
    5. Retract the tailing arm clear of the rotary.
    6. Make up the bolts (Vetco MR6E 900-1000ft/lbs). Ensure the dogs are fully
     engaged with the go/no go tool. Take a test lift and re-torque the bolts.
    7. Take the weight, open the spider and set the CMC at half stroke and run in
      the landing joint to close the telescopic joint.
    8. Lock the telescopic joint hydraulic locking dogs with 1500 psi, verifying that
         all the dogs are engaged. Air up the CMC and adjust the tensioners to the
    BOP landing weight.
    9. Inform all involved that the BOP is about to be unlatched.
   10. Air up the CMC to 50klb over pull and unlatch the wellhead connector
      observing the wellhead connector position indicator with the ROV. Once
      unlatched raise the travelling blocks allowing the CMC to stroke closed and
       hydraulic lock it when it bottoms out.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                54


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 55 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                        Issue Date
                                                         Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                         January 2011
                        Revision No.
                                           Manual Nr:
                              0
                        Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                    Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                       K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   11. When 5m above the wellhead move the vessel away from the well head (+
      or - 100 meters).
   12. Pull  the  telescopic  joint back  until  the  kill, choke and booster  line
     goosenecks are in position at the skidding beams for removal.
   13. Extend the skid beams and install the work platforms.
   14. Remove the gooseneck lock block retainers and lock blocks. Using the drill
       floor and cellar deck air winches remove the goosenecks and hang them
       off on the forward port side of the drill floor.
   15. Remove the MUX cable, conduit and riser fill line clamps as required.
   16. Remove the telescopic joint hydraulic latch and packer hoses. Plug the
      latch ports.
   17. Inform the moonpool and  pull the telescopic  joint  until the load  ring
     engages the diverter housing with 20klb over-pull. Lock the load ring
      support dogs and slack off some weight (40klb) to ensure the dogs are
        fully engaged, pick up to neutral weight again.
   18. Unlock the load ring rotating dogs and pull the telescopic joint until the
      inner barrel mandrel is just above the spider.
   19. Take up any slack in the MUX cables and conduit hoses.
   20. Close the spider and slowly lower the telescopic joint onto it.
   21. Slack off all the weight of the riser and back out the coupling bolts.
   22. Pick up the landing joint to ensure the joint is broken and continue picking
     up until the joint is clear of the joint in the rotary. Tail the joint out to the
      forward V door with the tailing arm.
   23. Install a protector on the pin in the rotary. Attach the starboard forward
     crane and the catwalk winch to the bottom of the joint.
   24. Ensure good communication between the driller, crane operator and air
     winch driver with the deck foreman in clear view.
   25. Take the weight with the crane and catwalk winch. Retract the tailing arm
     and lower the elevators booming out with the crane and controlling with the
      catwalk winch until the joint is horizontal.
   26. Set the flange on the deck and attach a drill floor air winch. Attach a drill
       floor winch to the elevators. Take the weight of the elevators and unlatch
     them. Lift them clear slacking off on the air winch as they clear the joint in
      the rotary, remove the air winch.
   27. Attach winches to the riser handling tool as required and back out the
      coupling bolts. Take the weight with an air winch and pull the riser handling
       tool off the joint with another.
   28. Install a protector on the pin.
   29. Ensure good communication between the crane operator and air winch
      drivers with the deck foreman in clear view.
   30. Pick up on the drill floor winch and crane then, guiding with the catwalk
      winch, lower the joint to the catwalk.

iii) Lay Out The Telescopic Joint:



                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                55


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 56 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                      January 2011
                     Revision No.
                                        Manual Nr:
                           0
                     Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                       Document Title:
                                                                 Procedures Manual
                    Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by

1. Remove the protector from the telescopic joint pin in the rotary and inspect
   the riser pin. Grease or oil as required.
2. Orientate the riser handling tool and stab it onto the telescopic joint in the
    rotary. Make up the coupling bolts to the required torque (Vetco MR6E
   900-1000ft/lbs). Ensure the dogs are fully engaged with the go/no go tool.
   Rotate the elevators to face aft.
3. Take a test lift, re-torque the coupling bolts and then take the weight, open
   the spider and inform the moonpool the string is ready to pull.
4.  Pull the riser at a steady speed. Ensure the MUX cable, conduit and riser
      fill valve hose reels are keeping up.
5. Set the packer box in the spider and set down the weight. Connect the
    latch hose, open the telescopic joint hydraulic lock dogs and stroke out the
   inner barrel.
6. While stroking out the inner barrel, clean and inspect it. Check for wear in
   the working area and stroke back in greasing the inner barrel liberally.
7. Apply 1500psi to lock the dogs. Verify all the dogs are fully engaged and
   remove the hose.
8. Continue pulling the telescopic joint and when the next joint is just above
   the spider stop hoisting and close the spider. Slowly lower the string onto
   the spider.
9. Slack off the full weight of the telescopic joint and back out the coupling
    bolts ensuring the dogs are fully retracted. Ensure the elevators face aft.
10. Pick up the telescopic joint to ensure the joint is broken.
11. Pick up the telescopic joint until it is clear of the joint in the rotary and tail it
   out towards the forward V door with the tailing arm.
12. Inspect the riser box and the kill, choke and booster line stabs. Change the
   seals as required. Grease or oil as required.
13. Install a protector on the joint in the rotary.
14. Attach the crane and catwalk air winch to the bottom of the telescopic joint.
   Take the weight with the crane and catwalk air winch and retract the tailing
   arm.
15. Ensure good communication between the driller and crane operator and air
   winch driver with the deck foreman in clear view.
16. Lower the elevators booming out with the crane and guiding with the
   catwalk air winch. Lower the box end to the catwalk then rig up the crane
   on the telescopic joint with a two leg spreader and unequal length slings to
   balance the top heavy loading.
17. Lift the telescopic joint with the crane to the horizontal position and lay the
   packer box down on the drill floor.
18. Attach a drill floor winch to the elevators. Take the weight of the elevators
   and unlatch them. Lift them clear slacking off on the air winch as they clear
   the joint in the rotary, remove the air winch.
19. Attach winches to the riser handling tool as required and back out the
   coupling bolts. Take the weight with an air winch and pull the riser handling
    tool off the joint with another.


                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                56


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 57 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011

                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   20. Install a protector on the telescopic joint pin and attach the drill floor air
      winch.
   21. Pick up the telescopic joint and using the air winches to guide it, lower it to
      the catwalk or directly onto the forward pipe deck.
   22. Vent down  the excess  air from  the CMC and dump  the sub sea
      accumulators as required.

iv) Pull Riser Joints To the Forward Pipe Deck:

    1. Remove the protector as required from the joint in the rotary. Inspect the
        riser connection,  kill, choke and booster  line stabs. Grease or  oil as
       required.
    2. Orientate the riser handling tool and stab  it onto the joint in the rotary.
     Make up the coupling bolts (Cameron RD 250-300ft/lbs, Vetco MR6E 900-
       1000ft/lbs). Latch the elevators and rotate them to face aft. Secure the
       latch with rope.
    3. Take a test  lift, re-torque the bolts and then take the weight, open the
       spider and inform the moonpool the string is ready to pull.
    4.  Pull the riser at a steady speed. Ensure the MUX cable, conduit and riser
             fill valve hose reels are keeping up.
    5. Disconnect the riser fill valve control hose when the fill joint is at surface
    6. When the next joint is just above the spider stop hoisting and close the
       spider. Slowly lower the string onto the spider.
    7. Slack off all the weight of the riser and back out the coupling bolts.
    8. Pick up the riser to ensure the joint is broken and continue picking up until
      the joint is clear of the joint in the rotary. Tail the joint out with the tailing
      arm.
    9.  Install a protector on the joint in the rotary as required.
   10. Clean and inspect the riser pins and boxes and kill and choke stabs. Install
     a protector as required.
   11. Attach the starboard forward crane and the catwalk winch to the bottom of
      the joint.
   12. Ensure good communication between the driller and crane operator and air
      winch driver with the deck foreman in clear view.
   13. Take the weight with the crane and catwalk winch. Retract the tailing arm
     and lower the elevators booming out with the crane and controlling with the
      catwalk winch until the joint is horizontal. Set the flange on the deck and
       attach a drill floor air winch.
   14. Attach a drill floor winch to the elevators. Take the weight of the elevators
     and unlatch them. Lift them clear slacking off on the air winch as they clear
      the joint in the rotary, remove the air winch.
   15. Attach winches to the riser handling tool as required and back out the
      coupling bolts. Take the weight with an air winch and pull the riser handling
       tool off the joint with another. Install a protector on the joint.
   16. Ensure good communication between the crane operator and air winch
       drivers with the deck foreman in clear view.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                57


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 58 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011

                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   17. Pick up on the drill floor winch and crane then, guiding with the catwalk
      winch, lower the joint to the catwalk.

v) Pull Riser Joints to the Riser Bays:

    1. Extend the riser tailing arm beyond the rotary and move the riser trolley
      towards well centre.
    2. Remove the protector from the joint in the rotary as required. Inspect the
        riser connection,  kill, choke and booster  line stabs. Grease or  oil as
       required.
    3. Orientate the riser handling tool and stab  it onto the joint in the rotary.
     Make up the coupling bolts (Cameron RD 250-300ft/lbs, Vetco MR6E 900-
       1000ft/lbs). Rotate the elevators to face forward.
    4. Take a test  lift, re-torque the bolts and then take the weight, open the
       spider and inform the moonpool the string is ready to pull.
    5.  Pull the riser at a steady speed. Ensure the MUX and conduit hose reels
      are keeping up.
    6. When the next joint is just above the spider stop hoisting and close the
       spider. Slowly lower the string onto the spider.
    7. Slack off all the weight of the riser and back out the coupling bolts.
    8. Pick up the riser to ensure the joint is broken and continue picking up until
      the joint is clear of the joint in the rotary. Tail the joint out with the tailing
      arm.
    9.  Install a protector on the joint in the rotary as required.
   10. Land the riser joint in the aft riser trolley, secure the joint to the trolley then
      extend the tailing arm to its full extent.
   11. Lower the riser joint allowing the aft riser trolley to move along the catwalk.
       Position  the  forward  riser  trolley  so  that  the  riser   will  overhang
      approximately 1.5m and land the joint on it.
   12. Attach a drill floor winch to the elevators. Take the weight of the elevators
     and unlatch them. Lift them clear slacking off on the air winch as they clear
      the joint in the rotary, remove the air winch.
   13. Attach winches to the riser handling tool as required and back out the
      coupling bolts. Take the weight with an air winch and pull the riser handling
       tool off the joint with another. Install a protector on the joint.
   14. Move the riser to the end of the catwalk and raise the catwalk bridge.
   15. Move the riser gantry crane over the riser, open the hooks and lower the
     beam onto the joint of riser. Close the hooks into the riser.
   16. Lift the joint off the riser trolley and transfer it into the riser bay racking the
        riser evenly on both sides of the catwalk. Open the hooks and position the
      gantry crane for the next joint.
   17. Lower the catwalk bridge and move the riser trolleys forward for the next
         joint.

vi) Control of the MUX, Conduit and Riser Fill Valve Reels:


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                58


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 59 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.

                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:

                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

    1. The MUX cables are very fragile and must be treated with great care.
    2. While the riser is pulled the MUX cables must be released from the riser.
    3.  Install walkways across the spider beams with  fall arrestors above for
      access to the riser kill and choke lines to remove the clamps.
    4. The MUX, conduit and riser fill valve hose reel controls are operated by the
      Sub-sea engineer or Electronics Technician.
    5. The hands free talk back system is to be tested before the operation starts.
    6. The cellar deck camera is to be focussed on the control reels.
    7. Ensure the manual brakes are released and that the reels are free.
    8. A warning must be given by the driller prior to moving the riser string.
    9. Always keep slack in the MUX lines.

vii) Recover the BOP:

    1. When coming off the spider with the last joint of riser, ensure the BOPs are
       properly orientated before coming into the splash zone.
    2. Inform the DPO and Engine Control Room that the BOP is about to be
      landed on the skid beams.
    3.  Pull the BOP on the last riser joint and termination joint until the top beam
       of the BOP is just above the skid beam support dogs.
    4. Extend the skid beams and lock them in place.
    5. Extend the BOP support dogs.
    6. Land the BOP on the support dogs.
    7. Back  out  the  flex  joint/termination  joint  coupling  bolts and   lift  the
       termination joint above the riser spider. Close the riser spider and set the
       termination joint on it.
    8.  Pull back the riser tensioner wires to allow the BOP gantry crane clearance
       to move.
    9. Move the LMRP test stump skid to the forward side of the moonpool and
       attach the BOP gantry crane to the LMRP.
   10. Block all the BOP functions, de-energise the seals and retract the BOP
       stinger.
   11. Unlock  the  riser  connector  (and   kill  and  choke  mini  connectors).
      Depressurise the pod as required.
   12. Lift the LMRP clear of the BOP and move the skid forward to the test
      stump skid. Land the LMRP on the test stump.
   13. Inform the DPO and Engine Control Room that the LMRP is to be skidded
       to the storage bay.
   14. Move the LMRP to its storage bay, following with the BOP crane.
   15. Connect the BOP gantry crane to the BOP and take the weight.
   16. Retract the BOP support dogs and pick up the BOP.
   17. Reposition the skid beams into the skidding position and engage the lock
       pins.
   18. Move the skid to well centre and land the BOP on the test stump.
   19. Move the BOP to the storage bay, following with the BOP crane.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                59


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 60 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011

                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:

                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and

   20. Lower the termination joint into the spider and back out the coupling bolts.
       Tail out the riser joint to the riser trolley and remove the riser handling tool
      as per section (v) of this procedure, “Pull Riser Joints to the Riser Bays”.
   21. Install the riser handling tool on the termination joint  if required and lay  it
      out to the forward pipe deck as per section (iv) of this procedure, “Pull riser
        joints To the Forward Pipe Deck”.
   22. Rig down the riser spider and gimbal.
   23. Install the adaptor ring, master bushings, insert bushings and hole cover.
   24. Change to 350 ton bails and elevators installing the link tilt cylinder pins as
       required.
   25. Install the mouse hole if required.

Post Task Actions:

    1.  After the BOP has been pulled, remove all handling gear and store it back
        in its original position.
    2. Clear away and clean all tools and store in the correct locations.
    3. Sub Sea engineer to ensure the MUX and conduit reels are locked.
    4. Secure the Riser and BOP gantry cranes and riser trolleys.
    5. Stand down standby boat as soon as over side operations are complete.
    6. Sign off all permits.

4.1.9 Disconnecting/Reconnecting the LMRP

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to ensure smooth and safe operation when
disconnecting and reconnecting the LMRP.

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried out prior and during the pulling of the BOP stack on Riser.

Responsible Persons:

Rig Superintendent / Toolpusher / Subsea Engineer / Driller / Crane Operator /
Deck Foreman

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

    1. A Pre-Job Meeting will held by the Rig Superintendent prior to pulling the
    LMRP and at every shift change with all the parties involved to discuss the
      operation and develop a safe plan of action. Job Safety Analysis available.
    2. Permit to work fully approved and signed with recommendations.
    3. Acquire weather forecasts and confirm a suitable weather window  is
       available for the operation.
    4. The BOP stack should not be pulled if rig motion is excessive (pitch, roll &
      heave) Crane limitations are a maximum of 40 knots wind speed and
     maximum of 1.5 degrees pitch and roll for handling the slip joint and riser.
    5. The rig is to be moved 100m off location as soon as possible after the
    LMRP has been unlatched and pulled clear of the guide funnel.
    6. The clear-call system between the rig floor and the moonpool is to be
       operational before the job begins.
    7. Work vests (correctly donned and secured) must be worn when working in
       or near the side of the moonpool.
    8.  All air hose connections must be secured with "R" clips and whipchecks.
    9. Safety harnesses attached to fall arrestors must be worn when working
      from the spider beams or from heights.
   10. PPE worn at all times.
   11. Pin protectors must be correctly secured before any riser is removed from
      the catwalk.
   12. Never run an air winch down through the rotary to the moonpool for man
        riding.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                61


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 62 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   13. Lifting gear fully inspected (check for appropriated colour coding, damage,
       malfunction, etc).
   14. All non essential personnel to keep clear of drill floor, raised catwalk and
        riser storage areas.
   15. Stand clear in the moon pool when the riser is moving through the rotary.
   16. Ensure good communication between driller, MUX reel operator and the
      crew working in moon pool.
   17. The LMRP should not be pulled if vessel motion is outside operating limits
        for LMRP handling, or weather window is not sufficient to pull the LMRP.
      Crane limitations are a maximum of 40 knots wind speed and maximum of
      1.5 degrees (full amplitude) pitch and roll for handling the slip joint, landing
        joint etc.
   18. The riser tensioners are numbered 1 to 10 clockwise from Port Forward.

Equipment Required:

    1. 500 ton bails and elevators
    2. Riser gantry crane
    3. Riser spider & operating remote panel
    4. Riser gimbal
    5. Riser trolley forward and aft. Run test the aft trolley
    6. Riser handling tools (Cameron RD and Vetco MR6E) on the drill floor
    7. BOP gantry crane
    8.  Drill floor tailing arm tested
    9. Spider beams
   10. Skidding system
   11. Diverter handling tool
   12. Load ring handling tool to remove the load ring from the diverter housing
   13. Riser tally to be available for Tool Pusher, Driller and Crane operator
   14. Fall arrestors and safety harnesses
   15. Rags
   16. Pipe wrenches, crescent wrenches
   17. Shackles with spare retaining rings/pins and spare slings
   18. Allen wrenches
   19. Pneumatic impact wrenches  with pressure  regulators  set below  the
       required torque of the riser bolts
   20. Torque wrenches set for the required torque of the riser bolts (Cameron
    RD 250-300ft/lbs, Vetco MR6E 900-1000ft/lbs)
   21. Vetco MR6E dog engagement go/no go tool
   22. Tools (2) to remove the MUX, conduit and riser fill valve hose clamps.
   23. Boat hook
   24. Set of tools, bars, spanners, hammers, brushes and rags
   25. Run test the riser trolley.

Preparation:


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                62


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 63 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

    1. Have the catwalk and drill floor air winches serviced and checked by the
      mechanic and check the wires are in good condition.
    2. Service the riser handling tools.
    3. Grease the riser spider and check the gimbal hydraulic system.
    4. Have sufficient air built up in the air accumulator bottles.
    5. Check/service all grub screws on the riser protectors.
    6. Jump the ROV and check the TV monitor on the rig floor is receiving a
     good  picture.  Position   it  to observe the wellhead connector  position
       indicator (give sufficient notice for them to reach the seabed).
    7. Clear the rig floor of any equipment not needed for riser handling.
    8. Service all moonpool air hoists including the manriding winches.
    9. Prepare a step down chart for reducing the riser tension in stages as the
        riser is displaced to sea water.
   10. Run and test an RTTS packer and pressure test  it. Then displace the
      casing and riser to sea water and flush out the kill, choke and riser boost
        line.
   11. Pull the packer running string and close the shear rams.
   12. Close the kill and choke fail safes and open the kill and choke isolation and
      the booster line valves.
   13. Clear any extraneous equipment from the riser bays.
   14. The slings for the diverter on the drill floor ready for use and if pulling the
    LMRP to surface also the slings for the telescopic joint.
   15. The termination joint, pup joints, crossover joint, riser  fill up joint, landing
         joint, and telescopic joint are stored on the forward pipe deck.
   16. The slick joints and buoyant riser are stored in the aft deck riser bays.
   17. Pull the catwalk air winch up to the drill floor.
   18. Lay out the Mousehole.
   19. Lay out the diverter as per section  (i) of procedure 4.1.8 – Pulling the
     BOP.





Personnel Requirements:

   Position                  Number     Location
   Rig Superintendent/Toolpusher   1            Supervision
    Driller                       1        DCR
   Assistant Driller               1             Control reels
   Derrickman                   1                 Drill floor-Riser trolley
   Floormen                    2                 Drill floor-Raised catwalk
   Floormen                    2              Cellar deck


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                63


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 64 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations

                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   Roustabouts                  2             Riser bays
  Crane Operator               1             Riser gantry crane
  Sub-Sea Engineer             1             Control reels

Unlatch the LMRP:

    1. Pick up the landing joint from the forward pipe deck as per section (iii) of
      procedure 4.1.7 - Running the BOP.
    2.  Install the  riser handling  tool make up the  bolts (Vetco MR6E 900-
       1000ft/lbs). Ensure the dogs are fully engaged with the go/no go tool.
    3. Remove the protector from the joint in the rotary. Clean the pin and box
     and oil/grease as required.
    4. Stab the landing joint slacking off the full weight of the joint to ensure the
      connection is fully engaged.
    5. Retract the tailing arm clear of the rotary.
    6. Make up the bolts (Vetco MR6E 900-1000ft/lbs). Ensure the dogs are fully
     engaged with the go/no go tool. Take a test lift and re-torque the bolts.
    7. Take the weight, open the spider and set the CMC at half stroke and run in
      the landing joint to close the telescopic joint.
    8. Lock the telescopic joint hydraulic locking dogs with 1500 psi, verifying that
         all the dogs are engaged.
    9.  Air up the CMC and adjust the tensioners to the LMRP landing weight.
   10. Block all the BOP functions, de-energise the seals and retract the BOP
       stingers.
   11. Inform all involved that the LMRP is about to be unlatched.
   12. DP to note the BOP heading and ships heading.
   13. Air up the CMC  to 50klb over  pull and unlatch the  riser connector
      observing the  riser connector  position  indicator with the ROV. Once
      unlatched raise the travelling blocks allowing the CMC to stroke closed and
       hydraulic lock it when it bottoms out.
   14. When clear of the BOP move the vessel away from the well head (+ or -
     100 meters).
   15. If required, pull the LMRP as per procedure 4.1.8 – Pulling the BOP.

 Re-connect the LMRP

    1.  If required, run the LMRP as per procedure 4.1.7 – Running the BOP.
    2.  If changing the ring gasket sub sea observe with the ROV and retract the
    LMRP connector ring gasket retainers.
    3. Ensure that the AX ring gasket falls clear of the connector.
    4. Inspect the riser connector mandrel with the ROV and install a new AX ring
      gasket on the mandrel.
    5. Adjust the riser tensioners and CMC to give 50klb down weight on the
      tensioners when the LMRP is landed.



                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                64


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 65 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

    6. While observing with the ROV position the ship over the BOP and lower
      the LMRP to a position 5m above the BOP.
    7. Set the CMC at ½ stroke and prepare to land the LMRP.
    8. Inform all involved the LMRP is about to be landed. DP will position the
       ship and adjust the heading to align the LMRP and BOP.
    9. While observing with the ROV run in and land the LMRP and blow down
      the CMC to give 50klb down weight.
   10. Re position the ROV, check the LMRP connector is fully open and then
      observe while the LMRP connector is latched. ROV to confirm.
   11. Air up the CMC and take a 50klb over-pull. Reduce the CMC weight to
      landing weight and adjust the riser tensioners to operating tension (with
      sea water).
   12. Extend the LMRP AX ring gasket retainers.
   13. Extend the BOP pod stingers and energise the seals. Set the functions as
       required and energise the BOP functions.

Post Task Actions:

    1. Carry out a full function test of the BOP on both pods.
    2. Pressure test the LMRP connector against the RTTS packer to 250psi and
      5000psi for 5/10mins.
    3. Displace the riser, kill and choke lines to mud staging up the riser tensions
      as required. Monitor the mud level in the riser to check for leaks.
    4. Run in and recover the RTTS packer flow checking the well once  it is
      unseated.
    5.  Pull out with the RTTS packer.
    6. Remove all handling gear to its storage area.
    7. Clear away and clean all tools.
    8. Sub Sea engineer to ensure the MUX and conduit reels are locked.
    9. Secure the Riser and BOP gantry cranes and riser trolleys.
   10. Sign off all permits.
   11. Run in with the next BHA.
