4.1.14   Drilling 81/2” Hole

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to ensure a smooth and safe operation while
drilling a 8 ½” hole section.

Scope:

The  following procedure describes the  activities  that are  to be carried out
immediately prior to and during the drilling of 8 ½” hole section.

Responsible Persons:

Rig Superintendent / Senior Toolpusher / Jr Toolpusher:

   •  Supervise and control all activities of this process.
   •  Ensure  that the  drilling and associated operations are carried out  in
      accordance with the defined operations programme and are conducted in
     a safe and efficient manner.
   •  Consult with the O.I.M. and Operator’s Drilling Supervisor regarding the
      progress of the Drilling and associated operations.
   •  Ensure that the unit has sufficient resources and supplies to perform
        drilling and associated activities as per contractual requirements.
   •  Confirm any calculations made are correct.
   •  Keep the O.I.M. informed of all events and occurrences likely to cause or
      have caused a deviation from the operational schedule.
   •  Measure and tally the next casing string.

Driller:

   •  Supervise and conduct all rig floor activities associated with this  process.
   •  Ensure that accurate tallies and depth control is maintained.
   •  Inform the on tour Toolpusher and Operator’s  Drilling Supervisor  if a
       deviation from the planned operations occurs.

Assistant Driller:

   •  Maintain pipe tally, drilling tally and BHA records accurately and up to date.
   •  Supervise and assist with drill floor, pump/pit room, shaker house, mixing
     and fluid conditioning operations.
   •  Pre-plan and make ready the correct tools, equipment and necessary
     manpower for forthcoming operations.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                96


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 97 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   •  Ensure that the Driller’s instructions to crewmembers are carried out.
   •  Bring to the attention of the Driller anything that may affect the safe and
     smooth running of the operation.
   •  Assist third party personnel involved in drilling operations.
   •  Check, measure document and inspect tubulars and drilling tools to be run
        in the hole.
   •  Assist with deck operations when required.

Subsea Engineer:

   •  Pressure test all BOPs and associated well control equipment.
   •  Responsible for running and setting the wear bushing.

Deck Crew:

   •  Transfer of equipment to and from the drill floor in the correct sequence as
       required

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

    1. Hold a Toolbox meeting. Review the procedure and JSA (Job Safety
       Analysis).
    2. Permit to work fully approved and signed with recommendations.
    3.  Lifting gear inspected.
    4. PPE worn at all times.
    5. Weather conditions in the working range of the vessel.
    6. Yellow & Red Alarms tested.
    7. Well control equipment tested and lined up for drilling.
    8. Back up safety valves ready for use on rig floor.
    9.  Kill sheet updated.
   10. Kill mud available.
   11. Non shearable procedure posted in the Driller Cabin.
   12. The drilling programme shall be available and instructions given in writing
       to the driller.
   13. The mud characteristics and volume ready as per program.
   14. The casing pressure tested as per the casing programme.
   15. 16 ¾” x 9 ⅝” wear bushing installed in the wellhead.
   16. All BHA  (Bottom  Hole  Assembly)  components  measured  with   all
      dimensions recorded on drawings and ready for picking up. This includes
      the drill bit and nozzles.

General:



                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                97


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 98 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.

                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations

    1. The minimum quantity of barite (or any alternative weighting material) held
     on board will be sufficient to raise the density of the mud system by at least
      1ppg, where:
             •  Mud system = hole volume (no pipe) + 150 BBLS surface
               volume.
    2. The minimum quantities of bulk cement and cement additives held on
      board will be sufficient to set at least two (2) 150m cement plugs in the
      hole section being drilled.
    3.  Drilling operations will be stopped if minimum stock levels are reached.
    4. Pressure test the BOPs and  all associated well control equipment as
      defined in the well control manual.
    5.  Install the 16¾ x 9 ⅝” nominal bore protector in the wellhead and lay out
      the  running/pulling  tool  in  the  derrick. The CMC (Crown Mounted
      compensator) will be used when pulling full bore tools through the BOP
     and slip joint shoe.
    6. Lay out the 12 ¼” bottom hole assembly.
    7. Make up and rack back the 7"  liner or casing hanger and pup  joint
      assembly  to  the  pack-off assembly, running  tool and sub sea plug
      launching system  with  the  Toolpusher, Operator  Drilling  Supervisor,
      Wellhead Service Engineer and Cementer in attendance.
    8. Make up and rack the cementing and dart-launching head to a stand of
        drifted landing string with the Toolpusher, Operator Drilling supervisor,
      wellhead Service Engineer and Cementer in attendance.
    9.  Verify that the float equipment is PDC drillable  if  it is intended to use a
    PDC bit.

Operation:

    1. Pick up and rack at least enough drill pipe to complete the next section.
    2. Make up and run in the 8 ½” bottom hole assembly (refer procedure 02).
      Test the MWD (Measurement While  Drilling) or LWD (Logging While
        Drilling) tools and down hole motor if used.
    3. Run in the hole with drill pipe until the bit is approximately 1 stand above
      the expected float depth.
    4. The CMC (Crown Mounted Compensator) will be open while the bit passes
      through the BOP, space out so the bit is not in the BOPs while the slips are
       set.
    5. Inform Bridge when the BHA is passing through the BOP.
    6. Carry out kick drills and conduct a choke drill (circulate up through the
      choke with the kill line open for pressure readings) prior to drilling out the
      shoe track.
    7. Pick up the drilling stand  if used and with no rotation, break circulation,
      then slowly run in and tag the plug.
    8.  Drill out the cement plug(s), float collar, shoe track and float shoe.



                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                98


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 99 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                      January 2011
                     Revision No.
                                        Manual Nr:
                           0
                     Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                       Document Title:
                                                                 Procedures Manual
                    Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by

9. Float equipment should be drilled at high flow rates with high WOB and low
  RPM and reamed several times to remove any cement, plug or float debris
   which may cause problems later.
10. Note and record the depth of the shoe and inform the Toolpusher and
   Operator Drilling Supervisor of any major discrepancy.
11. Clean out the 12 ¼” rat hole as the programme dictates.
12. Drill 3m of new hole, circulate and condition the mud and pull back into the
   shoe.
13. Where the BOPs are to be used for well control, as a minimum a
   formation  integrity  test should be conducted followed by slow
   circulation rates and choke line friction values.
14. Drill ahead and once the stabilizers are out of the shoe and in new hole
   then the rotary speed may be increased. Parameters will be at Operator
    Drilling Supervisor instruction.
15. Take any directional surveys as required by the programme.
16. The  riser may be boosted as  required  or as per Operator  Drilling
   Supervisor instruction.
17. Take slow circulating rates at the start of each shift and when the mud
   properties are changed.
18. Choke  line  friction values should be taken when the mud properties
   change.
19. Drill to the casing or coring point plus the rat hole required, normally 5-6m.
20. Take cores as required by the programme.
21. Prepare the 7” casing or liner.
22. Inspect the nominal seat protector and pressure test the BOPs at intervals
   specified in the drilling programme and Well Control Manual.
23. At the casing point, circulate the hole clean and make a wiper trip to the
   casing shoe if required.
24. If bridges or excessive fill are encountered, then repeat the wiper trips until
   the hole is in an acceptable condition to run open hole electric logs or the
   casing (refer procedure 04 for tripping parameters).
25. Flow checks are to be performed before pulling out, when the bit is at the
   lowest casing shoe and prior to the BHA being pulled into the BOP. Extra
   care to be taken when the stabilizers and bit enter the shoe.
26. It may be required to strap the pipe (SLM (steel line measure)) while
    pulling out of the hole, report any difference in the drilled depth to the
   Toolpusher.
27. If open hole logging is carried out, then a wiper trip may be required before
   running the casing.
28. Circulate and condition the mud as per the cementing program ready to
   run the casing.
29. If required by the casing programme perform a casing scraper run to clean
   the liner hanger setting point.
30. Prior to running a liner, drift the pipe in the derrick to be used to run the
    liner whilst logging. The outside diameter of the drift will be determined by


                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures                99


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 100 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

      the maximum outside diameter of the cementing dart or setting ball to be
      used.
   31. The operation above should be carried out before running in the hole for
      the final wiper trip prior to running the liner.
   32. Confirm dimensional clearances between the liner hanger and the wear
      bushing.
   33. If a liner is to be run “where possible” leave the 9 ⅝” wear bushing in
       position.
   34. Wash the wellhead area when pulling out of the hole to run the casing.
       Verify that the bit is below the well head and not in the BOPs. As an
       alternative the BOP can be flushed through the choke and kill lines.
   35. Continue to pull out. Rack back the BHA and break off the bit.
   36. Retrieve and inspect the wear bushing. Details regarding any wear as well
      as wear orientation will be recorded in the IADC report. Do not allow the
       string to rotate while recovering the wear bushing.
   37. Rack back or lay out the wear bushing running retrieving tool.
   38. Prepare to run the 7” casing or liner.

Post Task Actions:

    1. Clean up drill floor
    2. Ensure all stabilisers are clean after racking back
    3. Rig up the wireline for logging , if required
    4. Prepare casing equipment as per procedure 4.1.15 – Running and
     Cementing 7” Casing/Liner.
    5. Monitor well when out of hole
    6.  Slip and cut drill line if required
    7. Review the procedure and JSA for any improvement opportunities.

4.1.15  Running and Cementing 7” Casing / Liner

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to ensure smooth and safe operation while
running and cementing the 7” casing.

Scope:

This procedure describes the activities which are to be followed prior to and
during the running and cementing of the 7” casing.

Responsible Persons:

Rig Superintendent / Senior Toolpusher / Toolpusher:

   •  Supervise and control all activities of this process.
   •  To ensure that the operations are carried out in accordance with the
      defined operations programme and are conducted in a safe and efficient
      manner.
   •  Confirm that any calculations made are correct.
   •  Consult with the O.I.M. and Operator’s Drilling Supervisor regarding the
      progress of operations.
   •  Keep the O.I.M. informed of all events and occurrences likely to cause or
      have caused a deviation from the operational schedule.

Driller:

   •  Supervise and conduct all rig floor activities associated with this process.
   •  Ensure tallies and depth measurements are accurate.
   •  Inform the on tour Toolpusher and Operator’s  Drilling Supervisor  if a
       deviation from the planned operations occurs.

Crane Operator / Deck Crew:

   •  Supervise and conduct all deck operations associated with this process.
   •  Support all rig floor and moon pool activities as required.
   •  Bring to the attention of the on tour Toolpusher any occurrence that may
       affect the safe and smooth efficient running of operations.


Procedure:



                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               101


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 102 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir

Safety Precautions:

    1. Hold a Toolbox meeting. Review the procedure and JSA (Job Safety
       Analysis).
    2. Permit to work fully approved and signed with recommendations.
    3.  Lifting gear fully inspected (check for appropriate colour coding, damage,
       malfunction, etc).
    4. PPE is to be worn all the times. Chin straps to be worn on the drill floor.
    5.  All non essential personnel to be kept clear of Drill floor & Casing Bay.
    6. Ensure weather conditions are within the working range of the vessel.
    7. Yellow & Red Alarms to be tested.
    8. Ear protection to be worn in high noise areas.

Preparation Prior to Running Casing:

Consider the following items when preparing to run 7” casing:

    1. Read through and check Operator’s casing program.
    2. The casing shall be laid out on the pipe deck; a working bay should be
      kept beside the cat walk. An accurate count of all casing received will be
     made.
    3. Check that protectors are removed and that pin and boxes are cleaned.
    4.  All the Casing will be drifted, inspected, measured and an accurate tally
     made. The drift will conform to API Spec 5CT.
    5. Lengths and joint numbers will be painted on the casing if not done ashore.
      This to Include the pup joints but with letters (A, B, C etc).
    6. Any joints not to be run, rejected or which could not be drifted will be
       clearly marked and easily identifiable and recorded on the casing tally.
    7. The threads on shoe track joint,  will be thoroughly cleaned, dried and
      “Black bagged”. Shoe track joints shall be checked for debris, prior to
      being picked up.
    8. Check that the collars on the shoe track joints, the float and the shoe are
         all thread locked.
    9. Stop collars and centralizers can be fitted on deck, where possible.
   10. All lifting equipment will have current certification and colour coding. It will
     be inspected and made ready at the work site, with tag lines.
   11. Casing  handling equipment  to be prepared &  inspected  (side door
       elevator, single joint elevator, casing slip, rotary bushing inserts, casing fill
     up system etc). Test fit the elevators on all the grades of casing to be run.
   12. Single joint elevators will be connected to the v-door air winch on the rig
        floor and correct size confirmed by the asst driller.
   13. Dog collar clamp adjusted to size and hand slips with rope for securing the
      handles.
   14. Back-up casing tongs with long snub line set to casing size.



                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               102


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 103 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir

                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by

15. “Clamp on” air operated protectors shall be checked and made ready at
   the work site.  Dedicated airlines and attachments will be secured with
   whip checks.
16. A rope and hook will be hung on the v door ramp for returning the “Clamp
   ons” to the catwalk.
17. FMS (Flush Mounted Slips) power slips (if used) dressed with the correct
   size inserts, guides, will be lubricated, function tested and slung ready for
   picking up.
18. The Operator Drilling Supervisor will prepare a casing running list which
    will have been checked and approved by the Senior Toolpusher then
    distributed to the  driller, deck crew, crane operator, casing crew and
   cementing engineer.
19. The running list will be marked where joints enter the BOP and open hole.
20. While POOH, strap the pipe (S.L.M (steel line measure)) if required by the
   Operator Drilling supervisor. Result to be communicated to the Senior Tool
   pusher.
21. Wash and jet wash the wellhead area as per operational requirements.
22. Recover the wear bushing.
23. Drift the landing string.
24. Clear the Drill Floor of any excess equipment not required for the casing
    job.
25. Stabbing Board to be checked, as per the check list and lubricated. The
   Casing crew  shall be given  instructions  in  its use. To include  clear
    instructions on the hand signals required for the driller when the board flap
    is retracted.
26. A fall arrestor is to be installed above the Stabbing board prior to use.
27. A Pre-job Safety meeting with all personnel involved will be held prior to
   rigging up. These will also be held at shift changes. If the job changes at
   any time, for any reason, a meeting should be held to discuss the way
   forward. Remind all crew members to look up at all times, and ensure the
   stabbing board operators are fully aware of signals needed for the driller to
   confirm the elevator status and to confirm that the flap is retracted.
28. Rig up to run casing using side door elevators.
29. If FMS (Flush Mounted Slips) are to be used they can be run from the
    start.
30. Casing dope, barite and thread cleaning fluid, pressure washer, thread
   locking compound, stabbing guide and rags will be required.
31. Centralizers, stop collars, nails, hand tools and goggles if the centralizers
   were not previously installed.
32. Run test the power casing tong. Any time the power tong has to be laid
   down or not in use, the hydraulic power unit will be turned off and the
   pressure bled off. The Tong will be functioned to verify status.
33. All handling gear to have current certification and be within limitations of
   job requirement.



                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               103


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 104 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir

   34. All slings and shackles will have current certification, colour coding and
      inspected prior to use.  All shackles will be of 4 parts, ensure retaining
       rings/pins installed.
   35. Record all pit volumes. Inform the logging unit of line up and operation.
   36. Circulating swedge, check same as casing thread.
   37. Ensure thread compatible stabbing valve is made up. All crew members
         will be instructed in its use and the location.
   38. Space out of the Landing string calculated to give a workable stick up
      above the rotary table.
   39. Remove the rotary inserts and Master bushings and install the FMS slips if
      they are to be used. Install hole cover.
   40. Establish communications with the Stabber and check on the signals to be
      used.
   41. Clean the Derrick camera as required.
   42. Review and discuss the procedures and JSA (Job Safety Analysis) for the
       job.
   43. The crews are to be made aware that casing will continually be brought
       into the drill floor and as such they should not work with their backs to the
    V door.
   44. All personnel working on the drill floor will wear chinstraps.
   45. Pressure test the Cement hose and Lo-Torque valve assembly as per the
     cement program. This may be done off line.
   46. Plugs, darts installed in the cement head and plug launcher. This should
     be done  off the  Critical path where possible.  Personnel involved for
        installing plugs: Toolpusher, Operator Drilling Supervisor, Cementer and
      Wellhead Engineer.
   47. Cement Head made up and racked in the Derrick, one single up.
   48. The Hanger, running tool and sub sea launcher to be made up and racked
        in  the  Derrick.   If  required  the pack  off assembly can be  installed
      immediately prior to running.
   49. Dimensional and visual checks on the casing hanger and running tool.
   50. Prepare Mix water and Spacers as per the cement program. Be aware of
     dead volume. Check pit space for displacement. Possibly need a boat
       alongside.
   51. Check all lifting gear; ensure the rings are installed in the shackles.
   52. Install the side door elevators and rig up a tailing in rope.
   53. The safe and smooth running of a casing string depends upon safety
      awareness, good teamwork and excellent communication between  all
      personnel involved in the operation.

Running 7” Casing:

    1. Prepare the shoe joint on the catwalk with crane and sling 1m above the
      shoe and drill floor air winch and sling 2m below the collar.
    2. Ensuring good communication between the crane operator, winch driver,
     and deck foreman, hoist the shoe joint to the drill floor keeping  it slightly

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               104


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 105 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                      January 2011
                     Revision No.
                                        Manual Nr:
                           0
                     Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                       Document Title:
                                                                 Procedures Manual
                    Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   below horizontal to prevent  it swinging into the drill floor when  it reaches
   the top of the V door ramp.
3. Slowly bring the joint into the drill floor and land on the deck. Remove the
   protector and prepare the box as per the Operator Drilling Supervisor
    instructions for thread locking.
4.  Prior to lifting the shoe joint with the blocks install the #3 bushings inserts
   and install a hole cover.
5. Rotate the side door elevators to face the V door ramp and open them.
6. Pick up shoe joint with the drill floor winch and using the crane guide the
    joint into the elevators. When in the correct position drop the joint firmly
    into the elevators to turn them vertical causing them to close. Check they
   are properly latched and install the retaining pin.
7. Slack off and remove the drill floor winch and sling.
8. Ensuring good communication between the crane operator and deck
   foreman hoist the shoe joint simultaneously slacking off on the catwalk
   winch and crane keeping the float shoe clear of the deck.
9. When near vertical take the weight with the tailing rope, slack off the crane
   and remove the sling.
10. During Steps 11 through 16, the crane can place the next joints onto  the
   pipe deck. All personnel to stay well clear of the joint as it is being moved
    into position and landed, tag lines must be used.
11. Swing the shoe in to well centre and run in hole. Set the slips and fill it with
   mud, then pick up far enough to observe the mud draining out of the shoe.
12. Run the joint back in and set the slips. Check the level of the mud, to see if
      it has dropped. Secure the slip handles with rope (first 5 joints) and install
   a single joint elevator or safety clamp above the slips.
13. If using flush mounted slips these should be used from the start and no
   dog collar is required. Only the Casing crew will operate the FMS (Flush
   Mounted Slips) on instructions from the man on the brake.
14. Unlatch the elevators.
15. Mark off on the casing tally all the joints as they are run. Record any
   deviation in the running order to the Toolpusher and the Client rep.
16. Pick up the next shoe track joint (intermediate) using steps 1 to 9 keeping
      it secure with the tailing in rope. Do not hold the casing with hands inside
   the pin or box connections.
17. Clean the pin end thread (be aware of steel splinters on the threads) and
   check the box is clean then, when both joints are ready apply thread
   locking compound to the last third of the pin end thread.
18. Stab into the joint in the rotary table using the correct stabbing guide, slack
    off slightly on the elevators. The stabber will guide the casing from the
   stabbing board while it is spun in (unlatch the elevators if required). Do not
   hold the casing with hands inside the pin or box connections.
19. Pick up or swing in the power tong, spin in and make the connections up to
   the required torque/ mark as per the operator instructions using a back up
   tong and long snub line as required.


                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               105


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 106 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                      January 2011
                     Revision No.
                                        Manual Nr:
                           0
                     Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                       Document Title:
                                                                 Procedures Manual
                    Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by

20. Remove the power and back up tongs and rack back or lower to the floor.
  Be aware of pinch points when using the tongs and use the correct
   handles.
21. When the joint has been torqued up, the Stabber will latch the side door
   elevators and  signal the Driller to confirm and then signal again once the
   board extension is raised.
22. Remove the dog collar and pick up the weight, pull the slips and slowly run
    in the hole.
23. Record the pit volumes when starting and monitor the displacement while
   running casing.
24. Continue running the shoe track  joints applying locking compound as
   required (typically 3 joints).
25. Fill the casing with mud and pull back until the shoe is visible to check that
  mud passes through the float and shoe. RIH again and set the slips. Check
   the level of the mud; it should below the float collar.
26. Install the single joint elevators in a bridle long enough to reach the V door
   below the side door elevators. Ensure that there is a properly secured
    retaining pin to prevent the elevators from unlatching.
27. Install a Clamp-on protector on the pin end of the next joint and using a
     drill floor air winch fitted with a set of single joint elevators, bring the next
    joint into the V-door ramp.
28. Set the joint onto the stepped ramp (if fitted) on the V-door ramp. Slack off
   and remove the air winch. If the stepped ramp is not used then a sling will
   be used to lift and hold the joint until the pick up elevators on the top drive
   take the weight.
29. Remove the box end protector and dope the thread (this only to be done at
   the V door).
30. Latch the pick up elevators on the blocks onto the joint at the v door.
31. Pick up on the blocks and using tailing rope to control the joint, bring it into
   the floor. Keep it held back from the joint in the rotary table. Do not allow
   anyone to walk between the suspended joint and the one in the rotary or to
   walk under the joint as it is being picked up.
32. Remove the “Clamp-on” protectors with feet well out of the way and lower
   them down the V door with the rope and hook - do not throw them. Stab
    into the joint, in the Rotary table, remove the stabbing guide.
33. Slack off on the single joint elevators and clamp the casing tong onto the
    joint.
34. The Stabber (man on the stabbing board) will unlatch the pick ups and
   signal to lower the elevators depending type used.
35. The Stabber will rock the pipe to assist in getting the threads started. Once
   spun in the elevators will lowered over the box until the elevators are 0.3m
   below the casing collar. Once torqued up, latch the elevators and install
   the retaining pin.
36. Remove and secure the tongs. The Stabber will confirm that the elevators
   are latched and the stabbing board extension has been raised.


                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               106


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 107 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   37. Pick up and take the string weight pull the slips and RIH at controlled
      speed. Monitor the displacement. Check for losses or gains with the mud
       loggers.
   38. While making up the joints, the Crane crew will be preparing the catwalk
       with more casing. A joint can be pulled into the V door.
   39. Set the slips (secure the handles with rope and install a dog collar/pick up
       elevators as required) latch the pick up elevators on the joint in the V door
      ensuring the retaining pin is installed correctly.
   40. When there is sufficient weight in the string the back up tong will not be
       required.
   41. Check all the lifting gear on a regular basis.
   42. Continue running the 7” casing as per the  tally  filling every  joint and
      completely filling  it every 5 joints. 'Listen' for the height of the mud in the
      casing from a distance not over the top of the joint.
   43. Continue to run the 7” casing as per the above procedure, until the hanger
        is to be picked up.
   44. Confirm that the correct number of joints has been run and the correct
     number remains on deck.
   45. Pick up and make up the casing hanger assembly as instructed by the
      Wellhead Service Engineer to the last joint of casing and record the total
      weight of the casing string.
   46. Check  the  running  tool as  required by  the  Service  Engineer. The
      Toolpusher and Operator Drilling Supervisor must be on the drill floor at
        this time.
   47. Ensure the casing is full prior to running drill pipe.



Post Task Actions:

    1.  Prior to picking up the hanger and landing string, count the number of
        joints left on the deck. Verify the correct number have been run.
    2. Change over to drill pipe handling equipment.
    3. Clear the drill floor of any equipment no longer required.
    4. Clean up the drill floor.

Run 7” Casing On Landing String:

    1. Run the casing on the landing string.  Fill up the string on each stand.
    2. Open  the CMC (Crown Mounted Compensator) when  the casing  is
       entering the BOP.
    3. Inform DP when casing entering BOP.
    4. Control the running speed as directed by the Operator Drilling Supervisor.
    5.  Prior to going into open hole, make up the top drive and break circulation
     and monitor mud losses.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               107


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 108 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

    6. Work the pipe slowly through any ledges or tight spots and limit the set
     down weight. Inform Toolpusher and Operator Drilling Supervisor.
    7.  If unable to RIH make up the top drive and circulate down as required with
      low strokes. The Toolpusher must be on the drill floor.
    8. Monitor well closely for losses/gains.
    9. Approximately 10m above the wellhead space out and make up the dart
      launching head/swivel and space out to have the cementing head within
     10m of the drill floor while cementing.
   10. Install a lo-torque valve and cementing hose secured with safety wires as
       required.
   11. Make up the cementing hose and pressure test the surface lines as per the
      cementing programme.
   12. Verify the lo-torque valve and the cement head circulating valves are open.
   13. Open and adjust the CMC (Crown Mounted Compensator) as required.
   14. Using the mud pumps break circulation through the cement manifold
      continue to run in to land the hanger in the wellhead.
   15. Closely monitor the  pit volumes recording mud losses or gains while
       circulating and cementing.
   16. Circulate at least the casing and running string volume, or the casing
      annulus volume, whichever is the greater.

Post Task Actions:

    1. During the  circulation, inform the Marine Department  to  fluff up and
       pressurize the Cement silos.
    2. Ensure the cementer is ready.
    3. Mud balance and cups in cement room.
    4. Ensure surge tank line is clear.
    5. Communication system between cement unit and driller to be tested.
    6. Mud pits and lines cleaned and flushed ready for mixing spacers or cement
      mix water.
    7. Transfer lines from mud pits to cement unit cleaned and flushed.
    8. Mix the cement mix water.
    9. Mix the spacer when requested (ensure no loss or contamination).
   10. Record pit volumes.

Cementing 7” Casing:

Preparations prior to cement job:

    1. Double check calculations with cementing engineer and Operator Drilling
       Supervisor.
    2. Advise DP room and engine room that the job is about to start.  Double
      check weather forecast.
    3. Ensure mechanic and electrician are standing in case of emergency.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               108


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 109 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

    4. The following actions to be taken by the Derrick Man / Mud Supervisor and
      double checked by the Assistant Driller:
          •  Cement lines flushed and dried.
          •  Bulk air system fully operational.
          •  Ensure  rig  floor cement manifold and cement  lines have been
             flushed and ready for use.
    5. Mix water ready.
    6. Spacer lined up to cement unit.
    7. Cement batch mixer ready for use.

Cement Job:

    1. Line up the spacer and transfer to the cement unit as per the cementing
      program.
    2. Line up to transfer the mix water to the cement unit and also to circulate on
         itself to prevent deadheading of the pump.
    3. Cementer to pump pills and spacers as per the cementing program.
    4. Cementer to mix and pump the cement taking weights and samples as per
      the cementing programme.
    5. Release and displace the dart to the plug.
    6. Shear out the plug and displace the cement to the float collar.
    7. Do not over displace the cement by more than 50% of the shoe track
      volume. If the plug does not bump do not pressure test the casing and go
       to step 9.
    8. When the plug has bumped, pressure test the casing as per the drilling
      programme.
    9. Bleed off the casing pressure and check the float is holding, record the
      volume returned from the casing.
   10. Rig down the cement hose and lo-torque valve.
   11. Set the casing hanger seal assembly as per the wellhead service engineer
       instructions and pressure.
   12. Flush through the choke and kill lines and circulate the riser contents.
   13. Close a set of pipe rams and pressure test the seal assembly as required
      by the drilling program. Open the rams.
   14. Verify that all surface lines have been flushed and are cement free.
   15. Disengage the wellhead running tool from the wellhead using the CMC.
   16. Pull out, inform DP when clear of BOP. Use CMC (Crown Mounted
      Compensator) when pulling full bore tools through the BOP and slip joint
      shoe.
   17. Strap  the  landing  string (SLM) on  the  trip  out and  calculate  the
     measurement from the rotary table to the top of casing hanger if required.
   18. Verify that the ship is at normal drilling draft. Apply tide corrections and
       report this depth in the IADC report.
   19. Lay down the running tool.



                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               109


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 110 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

Cement and Set 7” Liner:

    1.  If running a liner do not pull the wear bushing.
    2. Run 7” casing (refer to “Run 7” Casing” procedure up to and including step
       44).
    3. Ensure the liner is full prior to running drill pipe.
    4. Pick up and make up the liner hanger assembly, as instructed by the
      Wellhead Service Engineer, to the last joint of casing and record the total
      weight of the casing string.
    5. Check  the  running  tool as  required by  the  Service  Engineer. The
      Toolpusher and Operator Drilling Supervisor must be on the drill floor at
        this time.
    6. Run the liner on landing string as per the Liner Hanger Service Engineer
     Programme (also refer to the run Liner on Landing string steps 1 to 8).
    7. At setting depth record the pick up, slack off and neutral weights.
    8. Open the CMC.
    9. Cement the liner (refer to procedure “Cement Job” up to and including step
      10) as per the cementing programme using the batch mixer.
   10. Set the liner hanger as directed by the Liner Hanger Service Engineer.
   11. Release the running tool  (if appropriate). Note the string weight to verify
       that the running tool is free. Record neutral weight, number of turns and
      the number of back turns obtained when the torque  is released after
       releasing the running tool.
   12. Pick up off the top of the liner with the setting tool and check for back flow
     up the drill pipe once the cement stinger has been pulled out of the pack
         off. This will indicate if cement is above the top of the liner.
   13. Pull the setting string above the calculated top of cement.
   14. Using the mud pumps reverse circulate to clear the string of cement
      ensuring any cement returned is dumped or circulate conventionally as per
      Operator Drilling Supervisor instruction.
   15. Pull out, inform DP when clear of BOP.
   16. Strap  the  landing  string (SLM) on  the  trip  out and  calculate  the
     measurement from the rotary table to the top of casing hanger if required.
   17. Verify that the ship is at normal drilling draft. Apply tide corrections and
       report this depth in the IADC report.
   18. Lay down the running tool.

Post Task Actions:

    1. Flush the cement lines clear of slurry.
    2. Purge the bulk transfer lines to the surge tank.
    3. Vent off the bulk tanks.
    4. Service the mud pump fluid ends and change piston and liner sizes as
       required
    5. Clean high and low pressure mud pump strainers.
    6. Check the remaining stock of cement.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               110


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 111 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

    7. Ensure that the surge tank is empty and made ready for the next job.
    8. Clean out the tanks used for spacer and mix water.
    9. Flush the mud mix lines.

Cement Calculations:

    1. Cement required (MT) = Slurry required (ltrs) / Yield (ltrs/MT).
    2. Mix Water required (M3) = Cement required (MT) x  Mix Water ratio (M3
      /MT).

Displacement:

    1. Volume of Landing String: Capacity of DP (ltrs/m) x Length of String (m) +
      Capacity of Stinger (ltrs/m) x Length of Stinger (m).
    2. Displacement: Volume String + Volume Stinger + safety margin.

4.2 NON PHASE DEPENDENT


4.2.1 Pipe Handing


 4.2.1.1 Changing the Bails and Elevators

Purpose:

The purpose of  this procedure  is to control the operation of  installing and
removing the elevators and bails.

Scope:

This procedure describes the activities which are to be followed prior to and
during installing and removing the elevators and bails.

Responsible Persons:

Rig Superintendent/ Toolpusher / Driller / A/D / Drill Crew

Procedure:

Safety Precaution and Preparation:

    1. Hold a Toolbox meeting. Review the procedure and JSA (Job Safety
       Analysis).
    2. Ensure the hole cover is in place, or the slips are set and covered  if
       required.
    3. Assemble and inspect the correct size ring spanners, pliers and slings.
    4.  If any lifting gear is required for the task, check it is fit for purpose, colour
      coded and the certification is valid.
    5. Tidy the drill floor.
    6. Ensure the bails are slung up and ready on the catwalk.
    7. Clear a space on the deck or catwalk to land the bails.
    8. Inspect and service the new bails.




Operation:

Remove the Elevators:

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               112


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 113 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by


    1.  If pipe in the rotary retract the blocks and rotate the elevators to face to
       port.
    2. Close the elevators and lower them to waist height. Remove the air hose
    3. Remove the split pins from the bolts in the bail retainers.
    4. Set the elevators on the floor and slack off on the bails until they come free
       of the bail lugs.
    5.  Pull the bails off the bail lugs and retainers. Raise the blocks clear of the
          drill floor.
    6. Attach a sling to the bail lugs and lift the elevators with an air winch to the
      storage area ensuring the sling does not slide off the bail lugs.

Change Out the Bails:

    1. Turn the pipe handler until it faces forward.
    2. Pass a sling through each bail and attach an air winch to the slings. Take
      the weight with the air winch and slack off on the travelling assembly until it
        is possible to reach the lugs on the solid body elevators. Do not allow
     anyone to walk under the bails while they are lifted.
    3. Land the lower end of the bails down on the deck and remove the air winch
     and slings.
    4. Remove the link tilt cylinder pins. Using a double wrap, sling up one of the
       bails (ensuring the sling is choked so that the bail will twist in the right
       direction) and attach an air winch. The second bail can be slung up at this
       time.
    5. Undo the nuts and remove the bail retainer bolts. Allow the retainers to fall
      open. This may be done from a stepladder.
    6. Pick up on the air winch and with a slight twisting motion (easier if the sling
        is choked the right way), remove the bail from the lug.
    7. Land the bail on a piece of timber on the drill floor and remove the sling
    8. Repeat with the second bail. Sling up the bails ready for lifting with the
      crane.
    9. Land the new bails on a piece of timber on the drill floor and remove the
       old bails.
   10. Sling up the first bail arm approximately 1/3 from the top with the sling
      choked the right way so that the bail will twist as required.
   11. Lift the bail, position it and twist it onto the lug of the solid body elevators
      ensuring that it is the correct way round.
   12. Close the bail retainer, insert the bolt, torque the nut and insert the split
       pin.
   13. Install the second bail repeating the operation.
   14. Install the link  tilt pins and retainers  if there are sleeves installed on the
        bails.
   15. Coil up and secure the air hose to the bail if not required.

Installing the Elevators:

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               113


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 114 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by


    1. Attach a sling to the bail lugs and lift the elevators with an air winch from
      the storage area ensuring the sling does not slide off the bail lugs.
    2. Set the elevators down on the drill floor to starboard of well centre.
    3. Remove the air winch and sling.
    4. Orientate the pipe handler to suit the elevators.
    5. Lower the bails until they are just above the deck.
    6.  Pull the bails outwards and  lift the bail retainers. Thread the retainers
      through the bail eyes and push the bails onto the bail lugs raising the block
        slightly assembly if required.
    7. Pick up the elevators to waist height and install the bail retainer bolts and
        split pins.
    8. Connect the air hose (if required) and test the elevators.
    9. Raise the elevators above the pipe in the rotary and extend the blocks.

Post Task Actions:

    1. Clear away and store all hand tools.
    2. Remove the used bails from the catwalk.
    3.  Periodically check the lug bolts on the solid body elevators to ensure that
      they are tight.
    4. Clean and check  all  lifting equipment used  for  this  task,  report any
     damaged equipment.
    5. Review the procedure and JSA.





                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               114

 4.2.1.2 Picking Up and Laying Out Tubulars under 61/2” O.D.

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to control the operation of picking up and laying
out tubulars under 61/2” OD.

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried out prior to picking up and laying out tubulars under 61/2” OD.

Responsible Persons:

Rig Superintendent / Tool / Night pusher / A/D / Derrickman

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

    1. Hold a Toolbox meeting. Review the procedure and JSA (Job Safety
       Analysis).
    2.  All tubulars to be inspected to DS1-Cat 5 Standard.
    3. Check tubular rotating hours comply with the contract prior to picking them
      up.
    4. Be aware of pinch points.
    5. Always stand behind air winches while operating them.
    6. Ensure that any lifting caps used are in good condition with current Lifting
        certification and that a swivel is installed.
    7. Ensure all shackles have pin retainers installed.
    8. Ensure that the threads are in good condition and not excessively worn.
    9. Ensure that the threads are compatible with the tubulars to be picked up.
   10. If pick up elevators are to be used check they are in good condition with a
       well secured retaining pin for the latch.
   11. Ensure the elevators fit the tubulars to be picked up.
   12. Ensure the slings, shackles and air winch wires are in good condition with
       current colour coding.
   13. Ensure the air winch brake is in good condition, properly adjusted and
       effective.
   14. All tubulars that are picked up will be drifted. Ensure the correct size of drift
        is available with a rope loop tail attached. Only one drift will be used for
      obvious reasons.
   15. Ensure that a tailing rope is properly installed on the Samson posts at the
      top of the v door ramp.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               115


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 116 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   16. Ensure the rope guard at the top of the derrick V door (protects against
       wire abrasion damage) is undamaged and secure.
   17. Install a rope and hook on the V door ramp for lowering the drift from the
          drill floor to the catwalk.
   18. Use a dog collar with low string weights (30klb typical).
   19. Ensure the catwalk is clear of personnel while picking up pipe.
   20. Do not attempt to move singles into the mousehole while the blocks are
      moving downwards.
   21. Protector boxes should be on the rig floor.
   22. A tally of the drill pipe on deck should be completed prior to picking up any
       pipe. This figure should be confirmed by the Tubular status board.
   23. A Fast line at the V-door maybe used to pick up the Drill pipe, all personnel
      should be aware of the potential hazards.
   24. Single joint elevators or lifting caps should be available at the site.

Operation:

Pick Up Tubular Using Mousehole:

All tubulars up to a maximum diameter of 6 ½” can be picked up from the catwalk
with a lifting cap or pick up elevators and air winch.

    1. Lay out tubulars on the catwalk.
    2. Measure the joints and mark the length on the pipe near the box end.
    3.  If using pick up elevators remove the box end protector and insert the drift
      then reinstate the protector (Install the lifting cap if used).
    4. Latch the pick up elevators to the pipe ensuring that the retaining pin is in
       place.
    5. Ensure the pin end protector is properly installed and tight (ensure no hole
        for drift to fall through).
    6. Pick up the joint from the catwalk with the air winch ensuring the deck crew
      are clear.
    7. Before bringing the pin end of the joint into the drill floor ensure the tailing
      rope is ready to catch the joint.
    8. Continue raising the joint simultaneously slackening the tailing rope until
      the joint is approximately half way to the mouse hole and the pin at waist
       height.
    9. Remove the pin end protector taking care not to drop it or the drift (ensure
            it comes out).
   10. Lower the drift down the V door ramp using the rope and hook.
   11. Wash the threads and apply dope.
   12. Lower the joint into the Mousehole.
   13. Remove the lifting cap if used or once the elevators come free of the upset
       unlatch them and send them down the V door ramp for the next joint.
   14. Note the length of the joint (fish neck length, O.D. and I.D. as required) in
      the pipe tally.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               116


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 117 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   15. Wash and apply dope to the box end thread and install a  lift nubbin as
       required. Make up chain tong tight with, a chalk mark across the joint.
   16. Lower the top drive to the correct height and using the link  tilt swing the
       elevators to latch on the pipe. Verify the elevators are latched and confirm
       to driller.
   17. Switch the link tilt to float and pick up the joint with the top drive and stab
      the pin into the box of the joint in the rotary.
   18. If new pipe walk  it in with a chain tong, torque  it up to the recommended
       torque, break it out and walk it out with a chain tong. Clean the re-dope the
      thread and make it up as normal.
   19. Spin in and make up the thread to the recommended torque using the iron
      roughneck.
   20. Pick up the string, pull the slips and RIH with the joint setting the slips
       gently.
   21. Open the elevators and prepare to pick up the next joint.
   22. When hoisting short subs such as x-overs and stabilizers use a  lift cap,
       swivel and v-door air winch to convey them to the drill floor. The sub can
      then be installed onto rotary pin where it can then be made up to assembly
      suspended from elevators.

Laying Out Drill Pipe Using Mousehole:

    1. Pick up one single with top drive.
    2. Set slips gently.
    3. Break out and spin out the joint.
    4. Hold the joint away from the rotary, clean and dope the pin end thread.
        Install a clean protector.
    5. Lower the joint into the Mousehole and when the elevators come free of
      the upset unlatch them but do not allow them to swing and strike the pipe
        in the rotary.
    6. Latch the elevators on the pipe in the rotary, pick up and pull the slips (Do
      not allow the slips to “ride” on the pipe while POOH singles).
    7. Wash the joint in the mousehole inside and out.
    8. Clean and dope the box end thread and install a clean protector.
    9. Attach the pick up elevators to the pipe.
   10. Pick up with the air winch until the joint is clear of the deck and push the
        joint out of the v door or alternatively shackle the catwalk air winch to a
       sling wrapped around the pin end of the joint.
   11. Check the catwalk is clear.
   12. Pull the joint out of  the V door with the catwalk air winch simultaneously
      lowering the joint with the drill floor air winch.
   13. Continue lowering the joint to where  it can be guided into position by the
      deck crew.
   14. Allow sufficient slack in the wire for the deck crew to unlatch the pick up
       elevators.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               117


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 118 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   15. Recover the pick up elevators and catwalk air winch wire to the drill floor
      ready for the next joint.

Post Task Action:

    1.  Slings, lift caps and lift nubbins used are to be returned to storage area.
    2. Winches to be re-spooled and secured.
    3.  All handling equipment, tongs, slips etc to be cleaned, checked, lubricated
     and stored.
    4. V-door gate to be installed on completion of task.

 4.2.1.3 Picking Up and Laying Out Tubulars above 61/2” O.D.

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to control the operation of picking up and laying
out tubulars above 61/2” OD.

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried out prior to picking up and laying out tubulars above 61/2” OD.

Responsible Persons:

Rig Superintendent / Tool / Night pusher / A/D / Derrickman

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

    1. Hold a Toolbox meeting. Review the procedure and JSA (Job Safety
       Analysis). A copy of the BHA to be displayed for the drill crew to familiarise
      themselves with the upcoming operation.
    2.  All tubulars to be inspected to DS1-Cat 5 Standard.
    3. Check tubular rotating hours comply with the contract prior to picking them
      up.
    4. Check all slings and shackles to be used are in good condition, with the
       correct SWL (safe working load) and current colour coding.
    5.  All slings are to be double wrapped around the tubulars.
    6. Ensure good communication between the drill floor, the deck foreman and
      the crane operator.
    7.  All tubulars that are picked up will be Drifted. Ensure the correct size of
         drift is available with a rope loop tail attached. Only one drift will be used
        for obvious reasons.
    8.  Install a rope and hook on the V door ramp for lowering the drift from the
          drill floor to the catwalk.
    9. Use a dog collar for low string weights.
   10. Use manual centre latch elevators.
   11. The Driller to have worked out how best to make up the bottom hole
      assembly  , bearing in mind handling requirements, the derrick height,
      what, if anything is likely to be changed out, the position of any stabilisers
     and forthcoming operations.
   12. The Drill Crew are to ensure that the handling gear for the bottom hole
      assembly is ready. Elevators, lift nubbins, drill collar slips with the correct

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               119


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 120 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

     number of segments, safety clamps / dog collars with the correct number
       of segments, dog collar spanner. Sledge hammer, chain tongs, additional
       insert bushings (if required), rig tongs are checked: - dies cleaned and in
     good condition, snub lines and breakout line secure and in good condition,
      extension tong jaws and pins, drill collar dope and brush. Master and insert
      bushing pullers at the worksite.
   13. The Assistant Driller to ensure the relevant cross over is made up to the
      stabbing valve and crewmembers informed of its location and use.
   14. The Driller to ensure the level in the trip tank is adequate to give accurate
      readings and the alarms on the flow meter and Trip Tank - empty / full set.
   15. All details of BHA to be recorded in BHA sheet. Drawings to be made of
        jars, accelerator and stabilizers. Enter these in the BHA drawing book.
   16. Lay out BHA on deck.

Operation:

Tailing in Drill Collars:

Tubulars above 6 ½” OD due to their weight are normally picked up horizontally
using an air winch and sling from the drill floor on the top end with the crane and
sling on the bottom end.

    1. Remove the box end protector and insert the drift. Reinstate the protector.
    2. Ensure the pin end protector is correctly installed and tight (ensure no hole
        for drift to fall through).
    3.  Install the lifting nubbin on deck, when possible, and make up chain tong
        tight. Mark across connection to enable crew to visibly see that the nubbin
        is not backing out. This can also be installed when on drill floor.
    4. Attach the drill floor winch to a sling about 2m from the box end.
    5. Attach the crane to a sling approximately 1.5m from the pin end and the
      catwalk winch shall be attached to a sling 1m from pin end.
    6. Ensure good communication with the  driller, deck foreman and crane
      operator and winch operator.
    7. Pick up with the drill floor air winch and crane simultaneously.
    8. Keep the joint a little below horizontal to prevent  it swinging into the drill
        floor at the top of the ramp.
    9. Once entered into the drill floor remove the box end protector and install a
         lifting nubbin (chalk mark across the tool joint).
   10. Rotate the elevators to face away from the V door, open them and kick out
      the bails.
   11. Position the joint below the elevators and pick  it up into the elevators to
       latch them ensuring they latch properly.
   12. Slack off the winch to give sufficient slack in the wire to remove the winch
       wire and sling.
   13. Lower the winch wire down the ramp ready to pick up the next joint.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               120


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 121 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   14. Pick up the top drive following as required with the crane and catwalk
      winch controlled by the deck foreman.
   15. When the pin end enters the drill floor is can be held on the catwalk winch
       while the crane and sling are removed.
   16. Remove the pin end protector taking care not to drop it or the drift (ensure
            it comes out) informing the driller that it has come out.
   17. Clean and dope the thread.
   18. Slack off slowly on the catwalk winch to stab the pin end into the joint in
      the rotary. Slack off and remove the catwalk winch and sling.
   19. Lower the elevators sufficiently so that they don’t bind on the lifting nubbin
      backing it off while the joint is spinning in.
   20. Spin in the joint with a chain tong and make up to the recommended
      torque with the iron roughneck or rig tongs and hydraulic catheads.
   21. Check the lifting nubbin tool joint remains tight before picking up out of the
        slips.
   22. RIH and set the slips. Install a dog collar above the slips as required.
   23. Back out the lifting nubbin and carefully lift it out with the elevators.
   24. Kick out the bails and lower the lifting nubbin to the drill floor and open the
       elevators.
   25. When hoisting short subs such as x-overs and stabilizers use a  lift cap,
       swivel and v-door air winch to convey them to the drill floor. The sub can
      then be installed onto the rotary pin where  it can then be made up to
      assembly suspended from elevators.

Tailing Out Drill Collars:

    1.  Install a lifting nubbin hand tight into the joint in the rotary (mark across tool
         joint).
    2. Latch the elevators onto the nubbin and pick up on the string.
    3. Set the slips on the next joint and install the dog collar.
    4. Break out and spin out the joint in the rotary.
    5. Place a bucket over the joint in the rotary.
    6. Clean and dope the pin end thread and install a clean protector.
    7. Double wrap a sling 1.5m from the bottom of the joint and attach the crane.
     The catwalk air winch can also be used to stabilise the joint.
    8. Ensure the elevators are facing away from the v door.
    9. Ensure good communication with the driller, deck foreman, crane operator
     and winch operator.
   10. Take the weight slowly with the crane and move the joint out of the V door
       while simultaneously lowering the top drive until just above the joint in the
       rotary.
   11. Double wrap a sling 2m from the box end and attach the drill floor air
      winch.
   12. Take the weight with the air winch and open the elevators.
   13. Lower the joint to the drill floor and remove the lifting nubbin.
   14. Clean and dope the box end thread and install a clean protector.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               121


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 122 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   15. In conjunction with the crane lower the joint to the catwalk.
   16. If the ship is moving excessively, causing the joint to swing keep the pin
     end low.
   17. Remove the bucket from the joint in the rotary.
   18. Install the lifting nubbin chain tong tight (chalk mark across the tool joint).

Post Task Action:

    1.  Slings, lift caps and lift nubbins used are to be returned to storage area.
    2. Winches to be re-spooled and secured.
    3.  All handling equipment, tongs, slips etc to be cleaned, checked, lubricated
     and stored.
    4. V-door gate to be installed on completion of task.
