 4.2.1.4 Tripping and Fill-Up Procedures

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to control the operation of tripping and fill-up
procedures.

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried on the rig floor, prior to and during the tripping and fill-up operation.

Responsible Persons:

Rig Superintendent / Tool / Night pusher / A/D / Derrickman

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and preparations:

    1. Hold a Toolbox meeting. Review the procedure and JSA (Job Safety
       Analysis).
    2. The space out for hanging off and shearing pipe should be displayed in the
        Drillers Control Room before the bit enters the BOP.
    3. The Driller will ensure that the rig floor is fully prepared to shut in the well,
     a suitable safety valve, threaded (or with proper connections ) to  fit each
      pipe connection included in the drill string, must be present on the  drill
        floor, in the open position ready for use with proper fittings and handling
       devices.
    4. The closing/opening wrench must be readily available for immediate use.
    5. The drop in dart should be ready for use and will previously have been
      checked to ensure it will pass through the safety valve.
    6. Always use the trip tank and pump (tripping in and out of hole).
    7. Before commencing a  trip out of the hole with  drill pipe, the following
        criteria must be followed:
          •  Circulate bottoms up as a minimum.
          •  There must be no lost circulation.
          •  There must be no indication of an influx.
          •  The mud density going into and coming out of the hole shall not
                differ more than 24g/l (0.2ppg).
    8. Before each trip the rotary slips shall be inspected for any worn or broken
       inserts and any repairs made. Replacement inserts should be available on
      the rig at all times.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               123


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 124 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by


Tripping:

    1. Always monitor DP alarms when the BOP is connected.
    2. Always be aware of the tool joint position for hanging off and shearing the
          drill pipe in case of a DP alarm.
    3. Always inform DP when entering or leaving the BOP with the BHA.
    4. The procedure for a non shear-able assembly in the BOP is to be posted in
      the Drillers Control Room.
    5. The Operator’s  Drilling Supervisor  will provide  written  instructions as
       required containing the necessary information about the  trip, prevailing
      pore pressure regime and tripping overbalance.
    6. Open the CMC (Crown Mounted Compensator) and  float the  bit and
       stabilizers through the BOPs while Running In and Pulling Out.
    7. Always pull the diverter packer (if installed) prior to pulling the BHA.
    8. The Operator’s Drilling Supervisor and Toolpusher shall be present on the
        rig floor at the beginning of every trip to check for proper hole fill and tight
       spots.
    9. The Driller will  fill out a trip sheet for each trip. The trip sheet from the
      previous  trip should be available for comparison. The  trip sheet shall
       clearly show calculated hole  fill volumes, actual hole  fill volumes and the
      cumulative discrepancy between the two values.  If any discrepancy  is
      noted, the Driller shall immediately inform the Tool Pusher and Operator’s
        Drilling Supervisor.
   10. The Mud Loggers will independently monitor the mud volumes and fill in a
         trip sheet during the trip. They will notify the Driller  if any discrepancies
      occur during the trip. If these are not resolved then the Drilling Supervisor
     and Toolpusher will be informed.
   11. Always stop tripping while filling and emptying the trip tank.
   12. As a general rule, if the hole fails to take enough mud, run the drill string
      back to bottom and circulate bottoms up.
   13. If Running In with a float in the string reduce the tripping speed, fill the pipe
      every 10 stands and break circulation at the shoe.
   14. Flow checks will be carried out at the following points:
          •  Before pulling out.
          •  At the lowest casing shoe (regardless of the fill-up status).
          •  Before the BHA enters the BOP stack.
          •  Whenever the hole does not give or take the correct volume of fluid.
   15. Prior to commencing a trip out, make sure that the mud is conditioned in
      order to have a minimum gel strength value within the desired values.
   16. Automatic slips shall not be used in open hole.
   17. Pull the  first 5-10 stands wet (or as required). Depending on the hole
       condition, a decision should then be taken to pump a slug, continue to pull
      out wet or to pump out of the hole to the shoe.



                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               124


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 125 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                      January 2011
                     Revision No.
                                        Manual Nr:
                           0
                     Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                       Document Title:
                                                                 Procedures Manual
                    Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by

18. The drill pipe should be slugged with a heavy pill. The volume and density
   should be determined by the Operator’s Drilling Supervisor based on the
   following factors:
       •  Density of the mud in the hole
       •  Mud rheology
       •  Capacity of the drill pipe
       •  Hole / bit depth.
19. As a general rule  it is preferable to pump a small volume of high density
   than a large volume of low density mud.
20. Under the following circumstances, the use of slug pill should be avoided:
       •  Shallow hole
       •   Possibility of damaging the reservoir with weighting agent
       •  When an increase in mud weight should be avoided in order to
         prevent mud losses and/or fracturing the formation.
21. The following formula is often used to calculate the pill’s volume

          •  Volume =     Drop x mud weight x DP Capacity
                                                  Pill Mud Weight

   Where:
       •  ‘Volume’ is in BBL.
       •  ‘Drop’ is in ft and represents the desired draw down in the string.
       •  ‘Mud Weight’ is in PPG.
       •  ‘DP Capacity’ is in BBL/ft.
22. The ‘Wiper rubber’ should be used when pulling out or running in (care
   should be taken not to lose the wiper while running in) to prevent any
   objects falling into the hole particularly while coring. Do not install the wiper
   rubber while tripping out the first 10 stands in order to observe the fluid
    level.
23. In an area where  formations  contain  (or are suspected  to  contain)
   hydrogen sulphide or other toxic gas, air breathing apparatus should be
   worn to observe the well at the rotary.
24. If the  trip tank pump  fails then the booster pump should be used to
    circulate  the  hole  continuously on  the  smallest  available  pit  or an
    alternative will be to use a mud pump. In this case the annulus will be filled
   with mud every 5 stands of pipe. Volumes will be monitored by isolating
   the suction tank and closely monitoring fluid volumes. A visual check of the
   annulus should ensure the mud level is dropping normally while pulling
   pipe.
25. If the drill pipe is pulled wet, drain the ‘Mud Saver Bucket’ into the trip tank
    for circulation back to the hole.
26. Any time a trip is interrupted, the hand tight installation of a safety valve is
   recommended.
27. The drill pipe shall be pulled and run in the hole at such rate as to prevent
   swabbing and pressure surges. Swab and surge pressures can breakdown

                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               125


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 126 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                      January 2011

                           0
                     Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                       Document Title:
                                                                 Procedures Manual
                    Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   the formation, or induce a kick. Swab and surge pressures are dependent
   on:
       •  Mud properties (viscosity and gel strength).
       •  Speed of the pipe travelling in the hole.
       •  The clearance of the pipe in the hole.
       •  The acceleration/deceleration of the pipe.

They will be calculated by the Mud Logger; every 150 m (500ft), prior to bit
changes and prior to running casing.

28. The string must not be rotated under the following circumstances:
       •  Tripping out a core barrel.
       •  Caving problems are encountered.
       •  Tripping out from thief zones.
       •  Tripping out from kick off zones (deviated holes, side-track, etc.).
       •  Pulling out running tools.
       •  Pulling string with a suspected washout.
       •  Pulling a broken string or fish.
29. The standard break out technique should be adopted in order to have all
   the tool joints in the drill string broken out and doped alternatively.
30. No welding/strapping  shall be allowed on the BHA unless expressly
   required by the Operator’s Drilling Supervisor for special reasons taking
    into consideration the particular tools and their position in the BHA.
31. In case of drag when tripping out, do not exceed a reasonable value of
   overpull (typically 1/2 of BHA weight in mud) unless the Toolpusher and
   Operator’s Drilling Supervisor are present. This value should be adjusted
    to hole conditions,  drill string design and stabilization and hole profile
    (vertical, side-track, directional). If necessary install the Top Drive to work
   the pipe and circulate to pass through the tight spot.
32. If drag  is encountered when  tripping  in,  install  the Top  Drive and
   wash/ream the zone free. Never attempt to push the bit through a ledge.
  No weight should be placed on the  bit during reaming. Torque, and
   sometimes pressure, are  the  only guide parameters  to perform  this
   operation. While reaming care should be taken to avoid making a new
   hole.
33. Always the record depths and over-pulls of troublesome zones on the
   IADC Reports.
34. Torque all joints to the API recommended value.
35. A short  trip shall be performed before tripping out of over-pressurised
   zones.
36. The following procedure shall be carried out for a short trip:
       •  Pull 5 to 10 stands at normal speed, making sure the hole is taking
          the proper amount  of mud (no swabbing). Use  the  trip  tank
          accurately.
       •  Run back to bottom.

                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               126


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 127 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

          •  Perform a flow check on the bottom.
          •  Circulate and check bottoms up.
          •    If an influx is detected, increase the mud weight as necessary.
          •  A second short trip may be required.
   37. Gauge accurately the residual diameter of the bit and stabilizers in order to
      plan a subsequent reaming operation or change the drilling string design if
     a PDC or diamond bit is scheduled. It is recommended to use a three point
     gauge ring if available.
   38. Wash and ream the last 3 stands to bottom or as Operator’s Drilling
      Supervisor instruction.

Tripping Procedures when using Oil Based Mud:

Gaseous fluids will dissolve in oil based mud at higher concentrations than in
water based mud. High temperature and pressure increase this behaviour. The
influx may remain undetected as it is circulated up the annulus until the pressure
in the wellbore  is equal to the bubble point pressure of the solution (1,000-
1,500psi). This could result in a sudden break out of gas close to surface.

Therefore when tripping out of the hole with oil based mud in a new reservoir
interval of unknown or unverified pressure (i.e. exploration or appraisal wells) or if
there is a predicted increase in pore pressure especially in a long shale interval or
there is a detection of significant gas levels in the mud, the following special
procedures should be followed:

    1.  Prior to pulling out of the hole, flow check the well.
    2.  Circulate bottoms up.
    3.  Pull out of the hole to the casing shoe or a shorter distance if desired, flow
      check and run back to bottom. Monitor hole volumes closely.
    4.  Circulate bottoms up.
    5.  If there are signs of a pit gain, increase the mud weight and perform a
       further check trip.
    6.  If there are no signs of influx after several trips under similar circumstances
      the procedure can revert back to normal.
    7.  After a round trip circulate bottoms up prior to any further drilling or coring
       to check for trip gas.

If a 7" liner has been run, the reduced clearance between the drill string and the
liner will increase the likelihood of swabbing, therefore pump out of the hole to the
top of the liner, flow check and pump a slug before tripping normally.

When pulling out of the hole with a tapered string (e.g. 31/2" - 5") additional 15
minutes flow checks will be performed:
   •  When the bit is at the liner overlap.
   •  Prior to the smaller diameter pipe entering the BOP.

Flow Checks:

When required, a flow check shall be performed as follows:

    1. During a trip, remove the wiper rubber.
    2. Pick up the drill string until the tool joint is in correct position above the
       rotary for the Upper Annular to be closed properly on the drill pipe body.
    3. Stop the mud pumps and charge pumps.
    4. Line up to the trip tank and start the hole  fill pump. Observe the trip tank
       indicator.
    5. The duration of the test shall be at least 15 minutes or whatever time is
      necessary to determine without any doubt that the well is static or flowing.
    6. Nevertheless, keep the time the drill string is not circulating as short as
       possible.
    7.  If flow is observed, close the BOP and begin well control procedures.
      Otherwise, resume drilling operations.

Displacement Volumes and Filling-Up Procedures:


Consider the following when determining displacement volumes and  filling up
procedures:
    1. Calculate the drill string displacement.
    2. Compare the calculated drill string displacement with mud or fluid volume
      recorded in the well.
    3. At the start and after every 5 stands ensure that the trip tank has sufficient
      volume to compensate for the displacement of the next 5 stands.
    4.  Start pulling out of hole and continuously displace with mud or fluid as the
          drill string pulls out.
    5. Record the volume for each stand tripped (10 first stands).  After the 10
         first stands, record the volume for each 5 stand. Continue until the bottom
      hole assembly (BHA) is pulled out. Check for gain or loss.
    6. Observe the mud indicator once out of hole.

Stand-Filling Practices:

Aban practice is to fill the well continuously.

If the trip tank pump fails then the booster pumps should be used to circulate the
hole continuously on the smallest available pit.

 4.2.1.5 Tripping Drill Pipe Using the Racking System

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to control the operation of tripping drill pipe using
the racking system.

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried on and above the  rig floor while tripping  drill pipe using the racking
system.

Responsible Persons:

Toolpusher / Driller / A/D / Derrickman / Drill Crew

Safety Procedures and Preparations:

    1. The  drill crew to  tidy away  all tools and equipment used during the
      previous operation.
    2. Refer to procedure 4.2.1.4 – Tripping and Fill Up Procedures.
    3. Chalk the numbers on the stands for running 5" Drill Pipe in the hole.
     Check the numbers tally with the number of stands in the fingers.
    4. Ensure there  is sufficient  drill pipe thread compound (dope) and dope
      brush at hand.
    5. Check the Crown -'O'- Matic / Crown Saver.
    6. Set the derrick camera at the correct height etc for tripping. Clean the lens
      as required.
    7.  Install the 5” automatic elevators on the bails.
    8. Select the drawworks gear to give the correct speed for the operation to be
       carried out (e.g. low for pull out of open hole).
    9. Clean the racker operating cabin windows.
   10. Check the communications system is operational.
   11. The racking arm operators to be in their respective cabs with the systems
       tested and ready for operation.
   12. Check the drill pipe slides on both arms are extended
   13. The aft fingerboard is normally used to rack the “working” drill pipe string
      as the jaws can be easily seen by the racking arm operators and the jaws
            fit between the stands better.

Run In Hole:



                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               129


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 130 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                      January 2011
                     Revision No.

                           0
                     Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                       Document Title:
                                                                 Procedures Manual
                    Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by

1. Pipe  will be run from the back row  first working fore and aft with the
   inboard row used last.
2. Place the upper racking arm alongside fingerboard to be used (this is
   protection against dropped stands), select the fingerboard (left or right) and
   switch the finger operation (open or close) to open.
3. Move the upper arm onto the stand of pipe to be run; close the jaw and
   open the finger latch by pressing the button on the row in use (press  it
   once for each finger to be opened).
4. Move the stand out 0.5m and wait while the lower arm is put on the pipe
   below the first tool joint and the jaw closed.
5. Pick the stand up with the lower arm and using good communication
   between the racking arm operators move it towards well centre keeping it
    vertical and stopping at the edge of the rotary.
6. Check the pin end thread for debris (drill pipe is magnetic) and inform the
    driller  if any work needs to be done. Dope the pin  if the boxes were not
   doped on the last trip out of the hole.
7. Turn the elevators to face the monkey board and open them.
8. Retract the blocks, engage high clutch and with full foot throttle raise the
   elevators up to the monkey board level.
9. Set the stabbing guide onto the tool joint in the rotary ready to receive the
   next stand.
10. Once the elevators are clear of the lower arm, stab the stand into the joint
    in the rotary with the lower racker and remove the stabbing guide. Open
   the jaw and remove the lower arm. Inform the upper racker when the jaw is
   open.
11. Activate the iron roughneck. Spin in the stand and torque  it up to the
   recommended torque. Retract and stow the iron roughneck. The Driller is
    to check the make up torque on his local torque readout.
12. When the elevators are at the correct height extend the blocks and move
   the upper racker towards well centre. Ensure the elevators are clear of the
    tool joint.
13. When the blocks are fully extended and at the correct height latch the pipe
    into the elevators with the upper arm. Confirm to the driller the elevators
   are latched before opening the jaw and removing the arm.
14. Do not latch an arm back onto the stand without informing the driller first.
15. Withdraw both racking arms (Inform the driller when each arm is clear) and
   prepare the next stand for running in the hole.
16. When the racking arms and iron roughneck are clear engage the low
   clutch and take the weight of the string.
17. If using automatic slips open them. Pull the manual slips. The drill crew are
    to use an underhand grip on the slip handles, this will enable them to let go
      if the slips become trapped between the rotary table and the elevators.
18. Run the stand in the hole at controlled speed using the Auxiliary brake
   (Elmagco) and band brakes as required. Ensure the slips cannot fall into
   the rotary


                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               130


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 131 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   19. When approaching stand down reduce the speed of descent to allow safe
       setting of the slips. The slips should be lowered into the bushings and not
      dropped which could cause damage to the pipe and slips.
   20. Close the automatic slips  if used with the string at slip setting height and
       with little or no downward movement.
   21. Continue lowering the elevators slowly to transfer the string weight to the
        slips.
   22. Once clear of the tool joint open the elevators and retract the blocks. When
       clear of the pipe, engage high clutch and full throttle to raise the elevators
        for the next stand.
   23. The drill crew are to keep the pipe numbered consecutively.
   24. Periodically clean the slip dies and apply grease to the backs of the slips
     on a regular basis.
   25. Periodically clean and inspect the iron roughneck tong dies.

Pull Out Of Hole

    1. The inboard fingers will be filled first working fore and aft.
    2. Place the upper racking arm alongside the fingerboard to be used (this is
       protection against dropped stands), select the fingerboard (left or right) and
      switch the finger operation (open or close) to close.
    3. Ensuring good communication between the upper and lower racking arm
      operators move the racking arms towards well centre but stop at the edge
       of the rotary (stand by position).
    4. With the elevators latched onto the stand in the slips engage the low
       clutch, take the weight of the string slowly and open the automatic slips if
      used. Pull the manual slips and pull the stand out the hole.
    5. Wipe or wash the pipe as required.
    6. As the last tool joint of the stand comes through the rotary, stop pulling the
      stand with the tool joint at just above slip setting height.
    7. Set the slips around the pipe and slowly lower the string and slips together
       to set the slips and transfer the weight of the string onto them. The slips
      should not be dropped which could cause damage to the pipe and slips.
    8. Close the automatic slips  if used with the string at slip setting height and
          little or no downward movement. Continue lowering the elevators until they
      are clear of the tool joint.
    9. Once the slips are set as confirmed by driller, extend the racking arms and
      close the jaws on the stand in the elevators. The lower arm jaw will be just
      below the first tool joint. Inform the driller when each jaw is closed.
   10. Simultaneously activate the iron roughneck to break out the connection
     and open the elevators and retract the blocks.
   11. When clear of the pipe, lower the elevators to the drill floor for the next
       stand. Stop above the lower racker if it is still at well centre.
   12. Spin out the stand. Retract and park the iron roughneck.



                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               131


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 132 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   13. Ensuring good communication between the upper and lower racking arm
      operators raise the stand out of the box of the tool joint in the rotary with
      the lower arm.
   14. Retract the arms away from well centre keeping the pipe vertical. Inform
      the driller when the stand is at the standby position.
   15. Continue to lower the elevators until they are at the correct height for the
      next stand, extend the blocks and latch the elevators onto the pipe.
   16. Move the stand into its allocated finger. Keep the upper arm 0.5m out from
      the last stand while the lower end of the pipe is positioned and set down.
   17. Lower the stand onto the setback with the lower arm ensuring the pin end
        is in the correct position. Open the lower arm jaw and retract the arm.
      Inform the upper racker operator when the arm is clear.
   18. The upper arm can then move the stand into its location in the fingerboard.
      Close the finger latch using the push button.
   19. Once the finger latch is closed open the jaw and retract the upper arm.
     Move both arms to the stand by position ready for the next stand.
   20. Engage low clutch, take the weight of the string slowly and open the
      automatic slips if used.

Post Task Actions:

    1.  After use latch the racking arms onto a stand of pipe. Do not leave them
      unsecured with the hydraulic pumps switch off.
    2.  Visually inspect the jaws and slides of both the upper and lower racking
      arms. Check they are not excessively worn and secure.
    3.  Visually inspect and grease the drive chains as required.
    4.  Visually inspect the stand  lift wire, cylinder and anchorage for wear or
     damage.
    5. Clean and thoroughly inspect the  slips, dies, handles etc for wear or
     damage.
    6. Leave the monkey board level clean and tidy with the racking arm latched
       to a stand of pipe.
    7. Clean the drill floor.

 4.2.1.6 POOH BHA

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to control the operation of pulling the BHA out of
the hole.

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried on the rig floor, prior to and during pulling the BHA out of the whole.

Responsible Persons:

Rig Superintendent / Senior Toolpusher / Toolpusher / Driller / A/D / Derrickman

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

    1. Hold a Toolbox meeting. Review the procedure and JSA (Job Safety
       Analysis).
    2. Work out how best to break the bottom hole assembly so that  it will rack
      back in the derrick, bearing in mind handling requirements, the derrick
       height, what,  if anything is likely to be changed out, the position of any
       stabilisers and forthcoming operations. Note: Jars will only be racked back
       at the top of a stand.
    3. Ensure that the BHA handling equipment is ready. Drill collar elevators,
        slips and safety clamps / dog collars with the correct number of segments,
     dog  collar spanner. Sledge hammer, chain tongs,  insert bushings  (if
       required).
    4. Check the rig tongs: - dies cleaned and in good condition, snub lines and
      breakout line secure and in good condition, extension tong jaws and pins.
    5. Master and insert bushing pullers at the worksite.
    6. Ensure the relevant cross over is made up to the stabbing valve and
     crewmembers informed of its location and use.
    7. Ensure the trip tank is full of mud and the alarms on the flow meter and trip
      tank are set.
    8. The  drill crew to  tidy away  all tools and equipment used during the
      previous operation.
    9. Refer to procedure 4.2.1.4 – Tripping and Fill Up Procedures.
   10. Ensure there are sufficient dope brushes, drill collar dope and pipe dope at
      hand.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               133


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 134 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   11. Have sufficient, correctly sized lifting nubbins for the stands to be racked
      back.
   12. The Driller to check the Crown -'o'- Matic / Crown Saver.
   13. Set the derrick camera at the correct height etc for tripping. Clean the lens
      as required.
   14. Select the drawworks gear to give the correct speed for the operation to be
       carried out (e.g. low for pull out of open hole, high for trip in the hole).
   15. Clean the racker operating cabin windows.
   16. Check the communications system is operational.
   17. The upper and lower racking arm operators to be in the cab with the
      systems tested and ready for operation.
   18. Rig up a snatch block at the back of the setback for pulling back the 9 ½”
          drill collars. The pull back sling will be 3T SWL (safe working load), double
     wrapped and choked on itself to prevent  it sliding down when it is put on
      the stand.
   19. Rig tongs with the tong line sensor (if used) on the make up tong snub line
     and the rod end facing the tong.

Pull Out Of Hole:

    1.  Fill in the trip sheet  (if drilling with riser) for each stand of Bottom Hole
      assembly pulled out of the hole.
    2. Lay out drill pipe from the last stand of drill pipe as required to give the
      best space out for racking back the BHA. Mark any joints laid out as
        “string”.
    3.  Pull out of the hole the HWDP. (Heavy Weight Drill Pipe) using the same
     method as for the drill pipe.
    4. On the last full stand of HWDP remove the automatic slips (if used) and
      master bushings, recover the pipe wiper and pull the diverter packer above
      the rotary. If there is a crossover leave it on the bottom of the stand.
    5. Replace the master bushings.
    6.  If the next stand is a mixture of HWDP and drill collars, use the drill pipe
      bushing inserts and slips.
    7.  If the next stand is all drill collars install the drill collar insert bushings, slips
     and a dog collar. Install a lifting nubbin on the top of the stand.

To rack back the stands, refer to procedure 4.2.1.7 – Tripping Drill Collars 8”
OD and below.

    8.  Pull the slips and if the jars are in the stand pull the insert bushings. Note
      Jars  will only be racked back at the top of a stand. Pull the mandrel
      through the rotary and replace the insert bushings. Set the slips with no
      weight to prevent tools etc from falling down the hole. Install the safety
      clamp.
    9. Continue pulling out of the hole, wiping or washing the stands and racking
      back as previous.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               134


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 135 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   10. Change out the insert bushings, slips and dog collar to suit the various
       sizes of drill collars in the hole.
   11. If pulling BHAs with stabilisers less than 17½” then only the insert bushings
     need to be pulled. Only pull out or replace the insert bushings with the
       single bushing pullers and an air winch.
   12. If pulling BHAs with stabilisers greater than 17 ½” the master bushings will
       also have to be pulled. Remove the hinge pin from the master bushings,
      keeping it away from the hole and using the master bushing pullers and air
      winch remove the master bushings.
   13. Pull the stabiliser through the rotary and replace the master bushings.
      Replace the hinge pin, again keeping  it away from the hole, and bushing
       inserts.
   14. Stabilisers that are to be racked back in the derrick above head height are
       to be cleaned of clay and compacted cuttings before they are racked back.
   15. Place a mat around the hole when using scrapers etc; to stop any hand
       tools falling down the hole.

If the penultimate stand is all 9 ½” drill collars or has a 9 ½” drill collar on the
bottom then to rack it back, refer to procedure 4.2.1.8 - Tripping 9 ½ drill collars.

   16. As the last stand of BHA is pulled have ready the correct size bit breaker
     and bit breaker plate.
   17. Remove the inserts and master bushings as required to allow the bit to
      pass through the rotary table. Once the bit is above the rotary table, push
      the stand to one side or retract the blocks and set it down on the Drill Floor
      (on timber) to prevent it from swinging around.
   18. Install the master bushings, bit breaker plate and bit breaker. Raise the
      stand just high enough to clear the top of the bit breaker, extend the blocks
     and stab the bit into the bit breaker.
   19. Set down the full weight of the bit stand onto the bit breaker ensuring that
      the cones have ' walked ' into the bit breaker cone slots.
   20. Engage the rotary table break out lock. Latch the breakout tong onto the bit
      sub just above the bit and make the tong bite. Break out the connection
       with the hydraulic cathead ensuring everyone is well clear of the tong and
       wire.
   21. Once broken make the tong bite again, select forward on the rotary and
       slowly back out the bit. Stop the rotary and remove the tong.
   22. If the stand is made up of 8” drill collar or less rack back as previously with
      the upper and lower racking arms.
   23. If the stand is made up of 9 ½” drill collars rack it back as per the previous
       stand.
   24. Using a  lifting cap and air winch remove the bit and set  it down in a
       suitable area so it can be cleaned off.
   25. Remove the bit breaker and bit breaker plate, install the insert bushings
     and put on the hole cover.

Post Task Actions:

    1.  After use put the racking arms onto a stand and stop the hydraulic pumps.
     Do not leave them unsecured with the hydraulic pumps switched off.
    2.  Visually inspect the jaws and slides of both the upper and lower racking
      arms. Check they are not excessively worn and secure.
    3.  Visually inspect and grease the drive chains as required.
    4.  Visually inspect the stand  lift wire, cylinder and anchorage for wear or
     damage.
    5. Rig down the air winch and snatch block.
    6. Clean and thoroughly inspect the  slips, dies, handles etc for wear or
     damage.
    7. Leave the monkey board level clean and tidy with the racking arm latched
       to a stand of pipe.
    8. Clean the drill floor.

 4.2.1.7 Tripping Drill Collars 8” O.D. and below

Although the upper racker can handle  drill collars up to 9 ½” OD the
maximum for the stand lift head is 8” OD. 9” or 9 ½” drill collars are handled
under a separate procedure

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to control the operation of tripping drill collars 8”
O.D. and below.

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried on the rig floor, prior to and during tripping drill collars 8” O.D. and below.

Responsible Persons:

Tool / Night pusher / A/D / Derrickman

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

    1. Hold a Toolbox meeting. Review the procedure and JSA (Job Safety
       Analysis).
    2. The  drill crew to  tidy away  all tools and equipment used during the
      previous operation.
    3. Refer procedure 4.2.1.4 – Tripping and Fill Up Procedures.
    4. Chalk the numbers on the stands in the order in which they are to be run in
      the hole. Check the numbers tally with the number of stands in the fingers.
    5. Ensure there  is sufficient  drill pipe thread compound (dope) and dope
      brush at hand.
    6. Have sufficient correctly sized lifting nubbins for the stands to be racked
      back.
    7. The Driller to check the Crown -'O'- Matic / Crown Saver.
    8. Set the derrick camera at the correct height etc for tripping. Clean the lens
      as required.
    9.  Install the 5” automatic elevators on the bails.
   10. Select the drawworks gear to give the correct speed for the operation to be
       carried out (e.g. low for pull out of open hole, high for trip in the hole).
   11. Clean the racker operating cabin windows.
   12. Check the communications system is operational.
   13. The racking arm operators to be in their respective cabs with the systems
       tested and ready for operation.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               137


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 138 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   14. Check the stand  lift slide is the correct size for the collars to be handled.
     Change if necessary. Retract the drill pipe slide.
   15. The drill collar fingers are the back or outboard row of each fingerboard.
     Due to the limitations of the handling system they may not necessarily be
      racked in adjacent fingers.
   16. Rig tongs with the tong line sensor (if used) on the make up tong snub line
     and the rod end facing the tong.

Run In Hole:

    1. Place the upper racking arm alongside the fingerboard to be used (this is
       protection against dropped stands), select the fingerboard (left or right) and
      switch the finger operation (open or close) to open.
    2. Put the upper arm onto the stand of collars to be run, close the jaw and
     open the finger by pressing the button on the row in use (press it once for
      each finger to be opened).
    3. Move the stand out 0.5m and stop. Put the lower arm on the stand in the
       zip lift recess and close the jaw.
    4. Pick the stand up with the lower arm keeping it no more than 15cm above
      the setback and using good communication between the racking arm
      operators move  it towards well centre keeping  it vertical and stopping at
      the edge of the master bushings.
    5. Turn the elevators to face the monkey board and open them.
    6. Retract the blocks, engage high clutch and with full foot throttle raise the
       elevators up to the monkey board level.
    7. When the elevators are at the correct height stop and extend the blocks.
     When fully extended move the upper racker towards well centre and latch
      the pipe into the elevators. Confirm to the driller the elevators are latched.
    8. Raise the elevators to take the weight of the stand, open the upper arm
      jaw and remove the arm confirming to the driller when clear.
    9. Do not latch an arm back onto the stand without informing the driller first.
   10. Continue raising the stand with the elevators until the pin is just above the
       height of the collar in the rotary.
   11. Check the pin end thread for debris and inform the driller if any work needs
       to be done. Apply dope to the threads and shoulder of the pin if not done
      during the trip out.
   12. Using the lower arm move the stand over well centre and then lower the
       elevators to stab the pin into the box guiding it with the lower arm.
   13. Ensure the stand lift slide is in the middle of the zip recess, open the jaw
     and remove the arm to the edge of the rotary (stand by position).
   14. If the vessel is moving significantly the top racking arm can be put back
      onto the stand as a guide.
   15. If using the top drive to spin in the drill collars keep a little weight on the
       elevators, activate the pipe handler rotation to make up and monitoring the
      weight gradually lower the elevators to allow for thread advance.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               138


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 139 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                      January 2011
                     Revision No.
                                        Manual Nr:
                           0
                     Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                       Document Title:
                                                                 Procedures Manual
                    Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by

16. If using a chain tong or iron roughneck continue lowering the elevators until
   they are well clear of the  lifting nubbin shoulder  to allow  for thread
   advance.
17. Walk  in  the stand  with a  chain tong  until snug and then  set  the
   recommended torque on the cathead control system and make up the joint
   with rig tongs and hydraulic catheads.
18. If using the iron roughneck activate and make up the connection to the
   recommended torque. Retract and park the iron roughneck. The Driller to
   check the make up torque on his local torque readout.
19. Inform the upper racking arm operator the connection is made up. Open
   the jaw of the upper racker and move the arm to the stand by position
   (Inform the driller when the arm is clear).
20. Both racking arms will then prepare to pick up the next stand to be run.
21. Before taking the weight of the string check the  lifting nubbin has not
   backed out.
22. When the racking arms and iron roughneck are clear engage the low
   clutch and take the weight of the string.
23. Pull the manual slips. The drill crew are to use an underhand grip on the
    slip handles, this will enable them to let go  if the slips become trapped
   between the rotary table and the elevators.
24. The slips are to be kept in hand as the stand is run in the hole to prevent
   them falling over on to someone's feet or from falling in to the hole and
    setting prematurely.
25. If there are stabilisers in the stand unlock and remove the bushing inserts
   (one at a time) until the last stabiliser is through the rotary then re-insert
   them, locking them in place.
26. Run the stand in the hole at controlled speed using the Auxiliary brake
   (Elmagco) and band brakes as required.
27. When approaching stand down reduce the speed of descent to allow safe
    setting of the slips. The slips should be lowered into the bushings and not
   dropped which could cause damage to the pipe and slips.
28. The slips should be set just under the top of the slip recess with a dog
    collar in the zip recess at low string weights.
29. Once the  slips are set as confirmed by  driller continue lowering the
   elevators slowly to transfer the string weight to the slips.
30. If using the iron roughneck, unlatch the elevators and break out the joint
   but do not use the pipe spinner to spin out the lifting nubbin as  it puts
   excessive stress on the roller bearings.
31. If using tongs break out the lifting nubbins with the elevators latched on the
    lifting nubbin. Break the connection and remove the tongs.
32. Walk the lifting nubbin out with a chain tong.
33. Lift the nubbin out of the box with an air winch or  if using the elevators,
    retract the blocks and rotate the elevators to face the riser catwalk. Install a
   protector on the nubbin and lower the nubbin to the deck.



                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               139


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 140 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   34. If using the elevators support the nubbin by hand or with an air winch and
     open the elevators and lay the nubbin down on deck and roll it or lift it to
      the sub rack.
   35. Turn the elevators to face the monkey board, engage high clutch and full
        throttle to raise the elevators to the monkey board level for the next stand.
   36. The drill crew are to keep the pipe numbered consecutively.
   37. Periodically clean the slip dies and apply grease to the backs of the slips
     on a regular basis.
   38. Periodically clean and inspect the iron roughneck tong dies.

Pull Out Of Hole:

    1. Place the upper racking arm alongside the fingerboard to be used (this is
       protection against dropped stands), select the fingerboard (left or right) and
      switch the finger operation (open or close) to close.
    2. Ensuring good communication between the upper and lower racking arm
      operators move the racking arms towards well centre but stop at the edge
       of the rotary (stand by position).
    3. Apply dope to the box end thread and shoulder of the collar in the rotary,
        install a lifting nubbin and torque  it up with the iron roughneck (50% of
     recommended torque is adequate).
    4.  If using the tongs latch the elevators on the nubbin before making it up.
    5. Put a chalk mark across the joint as a visual indicator should the joint back
        off while the stand is being made up.
    6. Extend the blocks and latch the elevators onto the lifting nubbin, engage
      the low clutch, take the weight of the string slowly, remove the dog collar (if
      used), pull the manual slips.
    7.  If there are stabilisers in the string ensure the diverter packer is out and
     remove the bushing inserts.
    8.  Pull the stand out the hole and wipe or wash the pipe if using water based
       or oil based mud, respectively.
    9. When the last stabiliser on the stand  is above the rotary replace the
      bushing inserts.
   10. When the last tool joint comes through the rotary, stop, with the connection
        just above slip setting height.
   11. Set the slips around the pipe and slowly lower the string and slips together
       to set the slips and transfer the weight of the string onto them. The slips
      should not be dropped which could cause damage to the pipe and slips.
   12. Continue lowering the elevators until they are clear of the lifting nubbin
       upset. Check in the derrick camera.
   13. Once the slips are set as confirmed by driller, put the racking arms on the
       stand. The lower arm jaw will be on the zip lift recess of the first tool joint.
      Inform the driller when each arm is on the stand with jaw closed.
   14. Either activate the iron roughneck to swing up and out to the well centre to
      break out the connection or use the rig tongs and hydraulic catheads to
      break it out. Park the iron roughneck.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               140


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 141 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   15. Back out the stand with the iron roughneck, pipe handler or a chain tong as
       required.
   16. Lower the stand-lift head on the lower arm to its full extent.
   17. Raise the elevators slowly to lift the stand out of the box of the collar in the
       rotary.
   18. Ensuring good communication between the driller and the upper and lower
       racking arm operators pull back slightly on both arms to clear the stand in
      the rotary and then lower the elevators until the pin end is just above (no
     more than 15cm) the master bushings, ensuring the stand  lift head does
      not take the weight prematurely.
   19. Take the weight with the lower racking arm and open the elevators.
      Keeping the pin end 15cm above the deck, move the stand towards the
       fingerboard keeping it vertical.
   20. Retract the blocks and lower the elevators to pick up the next stand.
   21. Position the stand keeping the top 0.5m from its finger and lower it onto the
      setback with the lower racker. Open the jaw and retract the arm. Inform the
      upper racker operator when clear.
   22. Move the top of the stand into position in the finger with the upper racker
     and close the finger latch. Open the jaw and retract the arm. Move both
     arms to the stand by position.
   23. Apply collar dope to the thread and install and torque up a lifting nubbin
      onto drill collar in the rotary table.
   24. Lower the elevators and when at the correct height extend the blocks to
      close them on the  lifting nubbin. Confirm to the  driller when they are
       latched.
   25. Take the weight of the string and remove the dog collar. The dog collar
     and spanner should be moved to a safe distance from the rotary.

Post Task Actions:

    1.  After use put the racking arms onto a stand of pipe and stop the hydraulic
     pumps. Do not leave them unsecured with the hydraulic pumps switch off.
    2.  Visually inspect the jaws and slides of both the upper and lower racking
      arms. Check they are not excessively worn and secure.
    3.  Visually inspect and grease the drive chains as required.
    4.  Visually inspect the stand  lift wire, cylinder and anchorage for wear or
     damage.
    5. Clean and thoroughly inspect the  slips, dies, handles etc for wear or
     damage.
    6. Leave the monkey board level clean and tidy with the racking arm latched
       to a stand of pipe.
    7. Clean the drill floor.

 4.2.1.8 Tripping 9½” Drill Collars

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to control the operation of tripping drill collars of
9½ O.D.

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried on the rig floor, prior to and during tripping drill collars 8” O.D. and below.

Responsible Persons:

Rig Superintendent / Senior Toolpusher / Night pusher / Driller / A/D / Derrickman

The lower racking arm normally used for racking and stabbing these collars
has been removed therefore the manual handling procedure is as follows.

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

    1. Hold a Toolbox meeting. Review the procedure and JSA (Job Safety
       Analysis).
    2. The  drill crew to  tidy away  all tools and equipment used during the
      previous operation.
    3. Refer to procedure 4.2.1.4 – Tripping and Fill Up Procedures.
    4. Chalk the numbers on the stands in the order in which they are to be run in
      the hole. Check the numbers tally with the number of stands in the fingers.
    5. Ensure there is sufficient  drill collar thread compound (dope) and dope
      brush at hand.
    6. Have sufficient, correctly sized lifting nubbins for the stands to be racked
      back.
    7. The Driller to check the Crown -'O'- Matic / Crown Saver.
    8. Set the derrick camera at the correct height etc for tripping. Clean the lens
      as required.
    9.  Install the 5” automatic elevators on the bails.
   10. Select the drawworks gear to give the correct speed for the operation to be
       carried out (e.g. low for pull out of open hole).
   11. Clean the racker operating cabin windows.
   12. Check the communications system is operational.
   13. The upper racking arm operator to be in the cab with the system tested
     and ready for operation.
   14. A maximum of two stands of 9 ½” drill collars are normally racked back in
      the last collar fingers of the forward and aft fingerboards.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               142


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 143 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011

                               0

   15. Rig up a snatch block to a pad eye or anchorage somewhere behind the
          drill collar to be run and in line with the collar and the hole (A pad eye may
     need to be installed for this). Reeve one of the  drill floor air winches
      through the sheave.
   16. The pull back sling will be 3T SWL (safe working load), double wrapped
     and choked on itself to prevent it sliding down when it is put on the stand.
   17. Rig tongs with the tong line sensor (if used) on the make up tong snub line
     and the rod end facing the tong.

Run In Hole:

    1. Wrap a sling around the bottom of the stand and shackle it to the air winch
      reeved through the snatch block.
    2. Support the sling until it bites and take up the slack in the winch wire.
    3. The Upper racker operator  will place the upper racking arm alongside
       fingerboard to be used (this is protection against dropped stands), select
      the fingerboard  (left or right) and switch the finger operation (open or
       close) to open.
    4. Put the upper arm onto the “bit stand”, close the jaw and open the finger by
      pressing the button for the row in use (press it once for each finger to be
      opened).
    5. Move the stand out to the edge of the rotary (stand by position) and stop.
    6. Open the elevators and turn them to face the monkey board.
    7. Retract the blocks, engage high clutch and with full foot throttle raise the
       elevators up to the monkey board level.
    8. When the elevators are at the correct height, stop and extend the blocks.
     Move the upper racker towards well centre and latch the lifting nubbin into
      the elevators. Confirm to the driller the elevators are latched.
    9. Raise the elevators slowly to take some of the weight of the stand and
       stop. Open the upper racker jaw and remove the arm confirming to the
         driller when it is at the stand by position.
   10. Do not latch an arm back onto the stand without informing the driller first.
   11. Continue raising the stand slowly with the elevators to take the full weight
       of the stand then start slacking off on the air winch until the bottom of the
      stand is at about waist height and just clear of well centre.
   12. Remove the air winch and sling.
   13. Lower the elevators and manually stab the stand as required.
   14. If the vessel is moving significantly the upper racking arm can be put back
      onto the stand as a guide. The driller must ensure that the arm is removed
      before the stand is run in.
   15. Break down or make up the bit stand as required.
   16. If using the top drive to spin in the  drill collars keep the weight on the
       elevators, select forward (make up function) on the pipe handler and
      monitoring the weight, gradually lower the elevators to allow for thread
      advance. Stop the rotation when the thread is made up.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               143


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 144 از 286 ]

   17. If not using the pipe handler lower the elevators an additional 15cm (to
       allow for thread advance), and make the thread up with the iron roughneck
       or a chain tong.
   18. Ensure all connections are made up to recommended torque.
   19. When the stand is ready run  it in pulling the bushing inserts and master
      bushings as required to allow the stabilisers to pass through the rotary.
   20. Set the slips and install the dog collar.
   21. Unlatch the elevators and retract the blocks and raise the elevators for the
      next stand.
   22. Break out the lifting nubbin and back it out with a chain tong. Lift it out of
      the box with the elevators or an air winch.
   23. Apply collar dope to the thread and shoulder of the collar.
   24. The second stand will be taken from the other fingerboard in exactly the
     same way by moving the snatch block to the other side.
   25. Periodically clean the slip dies and apply grease to the backs of the slips
     on a regular basis.
   26. Periodically clean and inspect the iron roughneck tong dies.

Pull Out Of Hole:

    1. The Upper racker operator will place the upper racking arm alongside the
       fingerboard to be used (this is protection against dropped stands), select
      the fingerboard  (left or right) and switch the finger operation (open or
       close) to close.
    2. Move the racking arm towards well centre but stop at the edge of the rotary
      (stand by position).
    3. Apply dope to the box end thread and shoulder of the collar in the rotary,
        install a lifting nubbin and torque  it up with the iron roughneck (50% of
     recommended torque is adequate).
    4.  If using the tongs latch the elevators on the nubbin before making it up.
    5. Put a chalk mark across the joint as a visual indicator should the joint back
        off while the stand is being made up.
    6. Extend the blocks and latch the elevators onto the lifting nubbin, engage
      the low clutch, take the weight of the string slowly, remove the dog collar,
        pull the manual slips.
    7.  If there are stabilisers in the string ensure the diverter packer is out and
     remove the bushing inserts and master bushings as required.
    8.  Pull the stand out the hole.
    9. Keep the slips in hand to prevent them falling over.
   10. Wipe or wash the stand as required.
   11. When the last stabiliser on the stand is above the rotary replace the master
      bushings and bushing inserts as required.
   12. When the last tool joint comes through the rotary, stop, at just above slip
       setting height.
   13. Set the slips around the collar in the slip recess and slowly lower the string
     and slips together to set the slips and transfer the weight of the string onto

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               144

      them. The slips should not be dropped which could cause damage to the
      pipe and slips.
   14. If using the pipe handler to back out the stand keep some weight on the
       elevators and ensure the pipe handler is unlocked. Otherwise lower the
       elevators until they are clear of the upset.
   15. Install a dog collar in the zip recess.
   16. Break out the connection with the iron roughneck or rig tongs and hydraulic
      catheads.
   17. Select reverse on the pipe handler and back out the stand (picking up
       slowly to allow for thread advance). Once backed out stop the pipe handler
       rotation with the elevators facing the monkey board. Alternatively a chain
      tong or the iron roughneck can be used.
   18. Wrap a sling around the bottom of the stand and attach the air winch to the
      bottom of the stand.
   19. Raise the elevators slowly to lift the stand out of the box of the collar in the
       rotary.
   20. Pull back the stand with the air winch, supporting the sling until  it “bites”
     and slowly lowering the elevators. Position the stand on the setback using
      the air winch and lower the elevators to set the stand down. Do not slack
        off all the weight.
   21. Ensuring good communication between the driller and the upper racking
     arm operator put the upper racking arm on the stand. Inform the driller
     when the arm is on the stand with the jaw closed.
   22. Continue lowering the elevators and once clear of the upset open them
     and retract the blocks.
   23. Lower the elevators to pick up the next stand.
   24. Pull the stand back and move  it into position in the finger with the upper
      racker and close the finger latch. Open the jaw and retract the arm.
   25. Slack off and remove the air winch and sling from the stand.
   26. Move the upper racker to the stand by position.



Post Task Actions:

    1.  After use put the racking arms onto a stand. Do not leave them unsecured
       with the hydraulic pumps switched off.
    2.  Visually inspect the jaws and slides of both the upper and lower racking
      arms. Check they are not excessively worn and secure.
    3.  Visually inspect and grease the drive chains as required.
    4.  Visually inspect the stand  lift wire, cylinder and anchorage for wear or
     damage.
    5. Rig down the air winch and snatch block.
    6. Clean and thoroughly inspect the  slips, dies, handles etc for wear or
     damage.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               145

7. Leave the monkey board level clean and tidy with the racking arm latched
    to a stand of pipe.
8. Clean the drill floor.

4.2.2 Top Drive


 4.2.2.1 RBU Connection while Drilling

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to control the operation of connecting the drill
pipes while drilling using the RBU (Raised Back Up).

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried on and above the rig floor to connect the drill pipes using the RBU.

Responsible Persons:

Rig Superintendent / Driller / A/D / Toolpusher / Derrickman / Floorhands

Preparation:

    1. Hold a Toolbox meeting. Review the procedure and JSA (Job Safety
       Analysis).
    2.  Drill crew to be in the racking arm control cabins with hydraulic pumps
       running.
    3. Pick up the next stand of pipe, clean and dope the pin end thread and
      prepare for the connection.
    4. Check the slips are clean and greased.
    5. Check dope and a brush are ready to dope the saver sub.
    6. Have ready the length of rubber hose for stripping the mud off the stand
     when the Driller back reams before making the connection.
    7.  Call  all necessary people involved (Shaker hand, Mud Eng, Directional
        Driller, Mud Logger etc) if required for surveys etc.

Procedure:

    1.  Drill to stand down with the elevator link tilt in the overdrill position. Keep
      the elevators as close to the drill floor as possible and ensure the drill crew
     do not walk under them.
    2.  Air up the CMC (Crown Mounted Compensator) slightly to reduce the
    WOB. Pick up off bottom slowly, allowing the CMC to stroke down to
       neutral position or bottom out and hydraulically lock it.
    3. Once clear of the floor move the link  tilt function to float to bring the
       elevators back towards well centre but do not latch them.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               147


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 148 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                      January 2011
                     Revision No.
                                        Manual Nr:
                           0
                     Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                       Document Title:
                                                                 Procedures Manual
                    Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by

4. Back ream and ream the stand as per the Operator’s Drilling Supervisor
   requirements, monitoring any drag, torque and weight differentials.
5. Take any directional or LWD (Logging While Drilling) surveys as required
    in the drilling programme under instruction from the directional driller, LWD
   hands, etc.
6.  Pull back to approximately slip setting height (6m) for the RBU (Raised
   Backup Unit), Stop rotation, shut down the pumps and monitor drain back
    to the active. Release any remaining torque in the string.
7. Shut off the audible alarms on the flow meter and pit volume totaliser.
8. Check the hole is free in slip area while taking up and down weights.
   Ensure the pump pressure is zero and close the upper IBOP.
9. Set the slips and reduce string weight to travelling assembly weight.
10. Lower the BUW (Back Up wrench) over the tool joint and close the gripper.
11. Select torque and activate the break out function to break the connection
   then activate the spin out function to spin out the top drive. The floating
    quill will compensate for thread advance.
12. Open the BUW gripper.
13. Pick up out of the tool joint, dope the saver sub and stop the spin out
    function.
14. Ensure the elevators are clear of the tool joint then hoist the travelling
   assembly to the monkey-board. Rotate the elevators into position for the
   derrick hand to put the stand in the elevators.
15. Unlock and lower the RBU to the tooljoint in the rotary and position the
   camera at monkey board level for the connection. Switch the top drive to
   spin in function.
16. Ensuring good communication with the racking arms raise the new stand
   approximately 30cm above the deck, and bring  it out towards well centre
   stopping with the bottom of the pipe at the edge of the rotary.
17. Using the upper racking arm latch the pipe in the elevators ensuring they
   are properly latched, then release the racking arm jaw.
18. Pick up the blocks to take the weight of the stand and with the lower
   racking arm stab the pipe into the RBU stabbing guide then release the
   racking arm jaw.
19. Continue to raise the stand with the blocks until the pin is above the tool
    joint in the rotary. Use the RBU stabbing guide to stab the pipe.
20. Continue lowering the top drive until the saver sub pin is a few inches
   above the tool joint.
21. Lower the BUW over the box to its full extent and then lower the top drive
    to stab the saver sub. After stabbing the saver sub slack off a further 6” to
   allow the floating quill to compensate for thread advance.
22. Close the RBU gripper.
23. Check that the top drive is set to torque and activate the spin in function.
   Spin in both upper and lower joints simultaneously. Once spun in select
   the make-up function to torque up both joints to the recommended value.
24. Raise the BUW fully, unclamp and retract the RBU ensuring it is locked in
   the park position.

                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               148


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 149 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   25. Open the elevators and tilt back off the pipe.
   26. Switch the top drive to drill, open the IBOP and pull the slips. Start the mud
     pumps bringing them up to speed slowly observing the pressures and
       returns. Un-lock the CMC, start rotation and zero the vernier when pump
      pressure has stabilized at the desired parameters.
   27. Note stand length and stand down, bit depth etc in driller’s pipe tally.
   28. Lower the bit back to bottom and  drill ahead with required parameters
       adjusting the WOB and CMC position as required.
   29. Reset flow meter and pit volume totaliser alarms when the flow stabilises.

Post Task Actions:

    1. Ensure the RBU is locked back in its parking position to avoid collision with
       blocks.
    2.  Periodically clean and check the dies on the RBU.
    3. Put the racking arms on the next stand to be run.
    4. Clean and tidy the drill floor.
    5. Derrickhand to inform driller when they are out of Derrick.





                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               149

 4.2.2.2 Drilling Stand Connection while Drilling

Should the RBU be damaged and unusable, an alternative is shown.

A drilling stand made up (from bottom to top) of 3 singles of drill pipe, a saver
sub, an IBOP, a pup joint (4-5m) with a saver sub on the top.

Approximately 3m above the top single a white mark is painted on the pup joint -
this is the bit depth at stand down +3m for the connection.

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to control the operation of making a connection
using a drilling stand.

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried on and above the rig floor to connect the drill pipes using a Drilling Stand.

Responsible Persons:

Rig Superintendent / Driller / A/D / Toolpusher / Derrickman / Floorhands

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

    1. Hold a Toolbox meeting. Review the procedure and JSA (Job Safety
       Analysis).
    2.  Drill crew to be in the racking arm control cabins with hydraulic pumps
       running.
    3. Pick up the next stand of pipe, clean and dope the pin end thread and
      prepare for the connection.
    4. Check the slips are clean and greased.
    5. Check dope and a brush are ready to dope the saver sub.
    6. Have ready the length of rubber hose for stripping the mud off the stand
     when the Driller backreams before making the connection.
    7.  Call  all necessary people involved (Shaker hand, Mud Eng, Directional
        Driller, Mud Logger etc) if required for surveys etc.

Operation:



                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               150

1   Drill to the stand down +3m mark with the elevator link  tilt in the overdrill
    position. Keep the elevators as close to the  drill floor as possible and
   ensure the drill crew do not walk under them.
2  Air up the CMC slightly to reduce WOB. Pick up off bottom slowly; allowing
   the CMC to stroke down to neutral position or bottom out then hydraulically
   lock it.
3  Once clear of the floor move the link  tilt function to float to bring the
   elevators back towards well centre but do not latch them.
4 Wash and ream the stand as per the Operator’s  Drilling Supervisor
   requirements, monitoring any drag, torque and weight differentials.
5  Take any directional or LWD (Logging While Drilling) surveys as required
    in the drilling programme under instruction from the directional driller, LWD
   hands, etc.
6  Shut off the audible alarms on the flow meter and pit volume totaliser.
7  Pull back to the connection at the bottom of the drilling pup. Stop rotation,
   shut down the pumps and monitor drain back to the active.
8  Check the hole is free in the slip area while taking up and down weights.
9  Ensure the pump pressure is zero close the upper IBOP.
10 Set the slips and reduce the string weight to travelling assembly weight.
   With the iron roughneck break the connection. Select torque on the top
   drive and activate the spin out function to spin out the drilling pup. The
    floating quill will compensate for thread advance.
11 Pick up out of the tool joint, dope the saver sub and stop the spin out
    function. Allow a few seconds for the mud to drain from the pup joint.
   Retract the blocks, simultaneously raising them to put the bottom of the
    drilling pup slightly above the level of the box of the new stand.
12 Extend the blocks to well centre once above the upper racking arm.
13 Meanwhile ensuring good communication between the racking arms raise
   the new stand just above the level of the pipe in the rotary and bring it out
    to well centre. Stab the pipe into the pipe in the rotary.
14 The iron roughneck can be used to spin in and torque up the lower
   connection.
15 Position the camera at monkey board level for the connection.
16 Move the top of the stand to well centre and slowly lower the top drive to
   stab the drilling pup into the new stand. After stabbing the saver sub slack
    off a further 15cm to allow the floating  quill to compensate for thread
   advance.
17 Either leave the iron roughneck clamped to the stand, or latch the breakout
   tong to the box of the tooljoint in the rotary and make it bite with the snub
    line tight and the rotary brake off.
18 Check that the top drive is set to torque and activate the spin in function.
   Spin in both upper and lower joints simultaneously. Once spun in select
   the make-up function to torque up both joints to the recommended value.
19 Release the racking arm jaws and retract the arms.
20 Switch the top drive to drill, open the IBOP and pull the slips. Start the mud
  pumps bringing them up to speed slowly observing the pressures and

       returns. Un-lock the CMC, start rotation and zero the vernier when pump
      pressure has stabilized at the desired parameters.
   21 Note stand length and stand down, bit depth etc in the driller’s pipe tally.
   22 Lower the bit back to bottom and  drill ahead with required parameters
       adjusting the WOB and CMC position as required.
   23 Reset flow meter and pit volume totaliser alarms when the flow stabilises.
   24 Put the racking arms on the next stand to be run.
   25 When the drilling stand is racked back in the derrick it should be positioned
      so that  it is always available for picking up to ream while running in the
       hole.

    1. Put the racking arms on the next stand to be run.
    2. Clean and tidy the drill floor.
