 4.2.2.3 Changing the Wash Pipe

Purpose:

The purpose  of  this procedure  is  to control the operation  of changing the
washpipe

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried out while changing out the washpipe

Responsible persons:

Rig superintendent / Tool Pusher / Driller / A/D

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

    1. Hold a Toolbox meeting. Review the procedure and JSA (Job Safety
       Analysis).
    2.  After draining the mud hose and lowering the blocks, turn off and lock out
      the drawworks.
    3. Place a “do not operate, man in riding belt” sign on the drawworks and top
       drive controls. Raise a Manriding Permit.
    4. The driller is responsible for ensuring that the sign is in place and that the
      drawworks are locked off as required.
    5. Ensure that there is no moving machinery e.g. travelling block, racking
      arms. Signs will be placed on the racking arm controls.
    6. Ensure that the air winch is certified for manriding and that the wire and
      carabineer are in good condition. Ensure that the brakes and clutch are in
     good working order. The crown sheaves will have been checked as per
      the daily PTW and JSA.
    7. Ensure that the air winch operator knows where the emergency shut off
      valve is. Only competent personnel should operate man riding equipment.
    8. Check for any unusual noise from the air winch.
    9. Check the condition of the harness and ensure it is worn correctly, take the
      time to properly adjust the straps.
   10. Ensure that the air winch operator, banksman and man going aloft are fully
      aware of the correct manriding signals.
   11. All relevant personnel to complete and sign off man riding checklist.
   12. Ensure the appropriate PPE and a hard hat secured with a chinstrap is
      worn.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               153


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 154 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   13. Ensure that any tools carried aloft are secured to the harness at the
       correct points. Do not secure tools to the adjusting rings or straps.
   14. If working aloft with hand tools, ensure that the area below the operation is
       barrier taped off.
   15. Hold a Tool Box meeting, review the procedure and JSA.

CAUTION:

   •  The Torque Boost should NEVER be used when tightening the washpipe
      nuts due to the excessive torque that  it generates.  It may be used for
       aligning the nut lugs only.
   •  The upper and lower nuts have left hand threads
   •  Apply a liberal amount of anti-seize to the threads of both nuts prior to
       tightening.

Tools Required:

   •  Washpipe installation and removal tool
   •  Washpipe wrench
   •  Small canvas or steel sling
   •  New washpipe
   •  Rags
   •  Grease
   •  Water hose
   •  Manriding winch and harness
   •  Warning signs and barrier tape.

Operation:

    1.  If the wash pipe failed during drilling, pull to the shoe or to the BOP as
       required.
    2. Raise the travelling assembly as high as possible, open standpipe bleed
        off valve and allow the mud hose to drain back.
    3. Once the mud hose has drained lower the travelling assembly as close to
      the drill floor as possible.
    4. Close the standpipe manifold bleed off and mud hose isolation valves.
    5. Open the upper IBOP and allow any mud to drain from the top drive.
      Mechanically isolate the drawworks.
    6. Remove the retaining pin bolts from the oil cooler assembly.
    7. Disconnect the air supply hose to the cooler at the quick disconnect.
    8. Swing the oil cooler assembly out on its hinges to clear the area.
    9. Wash off any mud around the washpipe area, removing all slip hazards.
   10. Pass a small sling through the middle of the washpipe installation/removal
       tool and wrench and using an air winch, lift them up to the top drive.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               154


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 155 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                      January 2011
                     Revision No.
                                        Manual Nr:
                           0
                     Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                       Document Title:
                                                                 Procedures Manual
                    Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by

11. When the packing is worn out, it may be that the threads on the lower nut
   have lost tension in which case, the lower nut  will probably break out
   before the upper nut.
12. To prevent the above, tighten the lower nut  first using the wash pipe
   wrench.
13. Install the washpipe wrench by fitting  it around the wash pipe and then
    sliding it down to fit onto the lower nut.
14. Set the torque limit to ZERO. Select FORWARD and advance the throttle
    to select very slow rotation (approx. 10 o’clock position).
15. To prevent the wrench arm from slamming against the side of the swivel
   housing, increase the torque limit VERY SLOWLY to 8000 ft-lbs.
16. Hold this torque for approximately 5 seconds, then zero the throttle and the
   torque, and remove the wrench.
17. Align the top and bottom wash pipe nuts to allow the coupling tool to be
    installed by rotating the top drive with the torque boost Make-up or Break-
    out).
18. Remove the pins from coupling tool to allow the tool to be opened.
19. Depending on the wash pipe nut design it may be necessary to remove the
   grease nipple before installing the coupling tool.
20. Install the coupling tool between the upper and lower wash pipe nuts.
21. Close the coupling tool and insert the pins (removed in step 18).
22. Set the torque limit to ZERO. Select REVERSE and advance the throttle to
   select very slow rotation (approx. 10 o’clock position).
23. Increase the torque limit very slowly to 8000 ft-lbs. Zero the torque limit
   and throttle, as soon as rotation is seen.
24. Do not fully unscrew the upper nut until the washpipe retainer tool has
   been inserted. It will also be used to hold the wash pipe in place until step
   29.
25. Should the bottom wash pipe nut break before the top nut, remove the
   coupling tool and perform steps 13 to 16, however, the make up torque for
   the lower nut should be increased to 9000 ft-lb, otherwise proceed to step
   26.
26. Remove the coupling tool and install the washpipe wrench on the lower
    nut.
27. To loosen the lower wash pipe nut, set the torque limit to ZERO. Select
  REVERSE and advance the throttle to select very slow rotation (approx. 10
   o’clock position). Increase the torque limit very slowly to 9000 ft-lbs. Zero
   the throttle and torque, as soon as rotation is seen. Any further rotation can
   be done by repeating this step again until the lower nut is fully unthreaded.
28. Remove the wrench and install the wash pipe retainer tool (P/N DT12826)
   between the wash pipe and the quill, to prevent the wash pipe from sliding
    into the quill, once the lower nut is loose.
29. Fully undo the bottom nut and then undo the top nut ensuring the retainer
    tool remains in place preventing the washpipe from falling down.
30. Lift the lower nut and slide the washpipe off the quill shaft. Ensure the ‘O’
   rings are also removed.

                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               155


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 156 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                      January 2011
                     Revision No.
                                        Manual Nr:
                           0
                     Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                       Document Title:
                                                                 Procedures Manual
                    Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by

31. Pass a small sling through the middle of the washpipe and using an air
   winch, lower it to the drill floor.
32. Thoroughly clean the sealing faces on the  quill shaft and gooseneck.
  Remove any excess mud.  Inspect the  sealing faces  for washes  or
   damage. Clean and inspect the threads.
33. Pass a small sling through the middle of the new washpipe, and using an
    air winch,  lift  it up to the top drive. Use rags to prevent the sling from
   damaging the seals.
34. Remove the sling and send the air winch back down to the floor. Check the
    ‘O’ Rings have not been damaged.
35. Liberally lubricate the threads on the quill shaft and gooseneck with anti
   seize compound.
36. Slide the new washpipe into position by holding and lifting the bottom nut.
   Ensure that the seals are not dislodged while inserting the washpipe. Start
   both nuts (by hand) until at least two full turns of threads are engaged.
   Note: Both threads are LEFT-HAND THREAD.
37. Install the coupling tool (as per steps 17-21)
38. Set the torque limit to ZERO. Select FORWARD and advance the throttle
    to select very slow rotation (approx 10 o’clock position).
39. To prevent over-torque, increase the torque limit VERY SLOWLY in small
   increments to 1000 ft-lbs until the nuts shoulder up.  If 1000 ft-lbs is not
   enough SLOWLY increase the torque limit to 2000 ft-lbs until they do.
40. Once the threads have fully shouldered up, SLOWLY increase the torque
    limit to 6000 ft-lbs. Hold this torque for approximately 5 seconds zero the
    throttle and torque.
41. Remove the coupling tool.
42. Check the washpipe seals for any leaks. If the seals are leaking, repeat
   step 40 increasing torque limit in 500 ft/lbs increments until seals are tight.
  Do not exceed the maximum torque value of 9000 ft-lbs.
43. If sealing of the wash pipe is not possible with the above maximum torque
   values, the packing may be defective and should be replaced with a new
    unit.
44. Once a proper seal has been accomplished, remove the coupling tool. This
   concludes the installation sequence.
45. Install the grease nipple if required.
46. Pass a small sling through the middle of the washpipe installation/removal
    tool and wrench and using an air winch, lower them to the drill floor.
47. Swing the oil cooler assembly closed, install the retaining pin and attach
   the air hose at the quick connect.
48. Remove all tools etc from the top drive. Check the air winch wires are not
   snagged.
49. Sign off the PTW.
50. Remove any isolations and spin the top drive to set the packing rings and
    distribute the grease.
51. Line up the standpipe to perform an integrity test on the washpipe. Close
   the IBOP and fill the mud hose with mud or water as required.

                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               156


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 157 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   52. Perform the integrity test to the maximum working pressure (5000psi) and
      confirm that the washpipe is not leaking.
   53. RIH and continue drilling as required.

Post Task Actions:

    1. Remove the warning signs.
    2. Remove any barriers.
    3. Remove any mechanical isolations from the drawworks.
    4. Ensure all the hand tools have been removed, inspected and returned to
      the workshop.
    5. Secure the manriding winch and take up the slack. Ensure that  it is not
      caught in the fingers or around the travelling block assembly.
    6. Clear away and inspect riding belts etc.
    7. Log the time that it took to change out the washpipe in the IADC report.
    8.  Strip down, clean and inspect the old washpipe. Redress with new parts as
       required and label and date as required.

Drawings:





                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               157


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 158 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
    Issue Date
                                 Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
       January 2011
    Revision No.

 4.2.2.4 Changing the Top Drive Lower Well Control Valve

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to control the operation of changing the top drive
(LWCV) lower well control valve.

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried out while changing out the (LWCV).

Responsible persons:

Rig superintendent / Tool Pusher / Driller / A/D

Procedure:

Safety Precautions:

Hold a Tool Box meeting, review the procedure and TRA.

Tools Required:

   •  Allen wrench
   •   Drill collar dope
   •   Drill pipe dope
   •  Dope brush
   •  Rags.

Operation:

    1.  Fully extend the bails ensuring the pipe handler is locked.
    2. Lower the top drive as close as possible to the drill floor.
    3.  If only the LWRC is to be changed remove the 12 socket head cap screws
     and the clamp from the top joint of the LWCV.
    4. Pick up the top drive and lower the link tilt to vertical.
    5. Clamp the back up wrench onto the LWCV.
    6. Break out the connection with the top drive using the torque boost as
       required.
    7. Lower the top drive, picking up the links.
    8. Spin out the top drive.
    9. Set the LWCV onto timber or a mat and open the back up wrench gripper.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               161


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 162 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   10. Support the LWCV by hand and lift the top drive clear.
   11. Inspect the threads on the crossover.
   12. Prepare the new LWCV and apply drill collar dope to the threads.
   13. Set the new LWCV on timber or a mat and lower the top drive carefully to
      stab into the LWCV.
   14. Start the LWCV thread by hand until fully made up.
   15. Clamp the back up wrench onto the LWCV and torque up the 4 ½”IF
      connection to the required torque with the top drive, using the torque boost
            if required.
   16. Install the joint clamp ensuring the anti rotation dogs are fully engaged and
       tighten the 12 socket head cap screws to 198ftlbs.
   17. If removing the complete LWCV and crossover assembly, loosen and
     remove the 6 cap screws on the joint clamp above the crossover.
   18. Remove the upper joint clamp assembly.
   19. Breakout the connection following steps 4-8.
   20. Install the new assembly following steps 10-12.
   21. Clamp the back up wrench onto the LWCV and torque up the 7 5/8” Reg
      connection to the required torque with the top drive, using the torque boost
            if required.
   22. Install the joint clamp ensuring the anti rotation dogs are fully engaged and
       tighten the 6 socket head cap screws to 198ftlbs.
   23. To check the LWCV is fully made up, carry out steps 3, 13 and 14.

Post Task Actions:

    1. Send the old LWCV for re-cutting or overhaul as required.
    2. Apply dope to the pin end of the LWCV. Make up and break out the
      connection carefully.
    3. Pressure test the LWCV upwards to 250 and 10,000 psi for 5 and 10
      minutes respectively.
    4. Pressure test the LWCV downwards to 250 and 5000psi for 5 and 10
      minutes respectively.

4.2.3 Casing


 4.2.3.1 Transfer Tubulars to & from a Supply Vessel

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to control the operation of transferring casing
and tubulars to and from a supply vessel.

Scope:

This procedure describes the activities which are to be followed prior to and
during the transfer of casing and tubulars form and to a supply vessel.

Responsible Persons:

OIM / Mate / Deck Foreman / Crane Operator / Deck Crew

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and Preparations:

    1. Hold a Toolbox meeting. Review the procedure and JSA (Job Safety
       Analysis).
    2. Be aware of pinch points, such as getting the hands, fingers and feet
      trapped between  tubulars. Beware  of  the movement  of  the  vessel.
     Remember to chock all tubulars.
    3. Ensure the pipe bays are free from debris and trip hazards.
    4. Inspect and prepare the crane.
    5. Inspect and prepare the dedicated certificated, colour coded lifting gear.
     Remove from service and cut any damaged equipment.
    6. Inspect and prepare the taglines.
    7.  If the tubulars are to be laid out and measured while loading inspect and
      prepare the rig tape. Have a paint brush and white paint for marking the
      casing and prepare a tally sheet.
    8. Prepare transit slings or area for transit sling storage.
    9.  Identify the potential hazards.
   10. Appoint a dedicated deck foreman.
   11. Establish communication.
   12. Prepare hand tools and bulldog grips.
   13. Prepare wood and wooden wedges.



                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               164


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 165 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   14. Ensure the area is clear of non-essential personnel, including the area
      between the pipe racks and the ships side. This will eliminate the risk of
      personnel walking underneath a suspended load.

Operation:

Back loading to a Supply Vessel:

    1. A manifest with the correct tally of the joints to be back loaded should be
      provided by the Storekeeper.
    2. Bundle the tubulars ready for back loading.  Each sling will be double
     wrapped and choked in the same direction.
    3. A maximum of three joints to a bundle of 13⅜” Casing with 5 ton S.W.L
       (safe working load) transit slings or a maximum of two joints per bundle
       with 3 ton S.W.L. transit slings.
    4. A maximum of five joints to a bundle of 9⅝” casing with 5 ton S.W.L (safe
      working load) transit slings or a maximum of three joints per bundle with 3
      ton S.W.L. transit slings.
    5. On smaller sizes of casing the limitation is the number of joints that can be
       safely bundled (usually between 5 and 10).
    6. For 5” drill pipe the maximum is normally 10 joints to a bundle.
    7. For HWDP the maximum is normally 5 - 7 joints per bundle.
    8.  Drill collars and casing larger than 13⅜” are normally slung singly but may
     be picked up in multiples.
    9.  Install the slings on the required number of joints, attach the crane and
       pick up sufficiently to bundle up the tubulars. Install bulldog grips on the
       slings to prevent the eye from sliding back along the choked sling when it
        is released.
   10. Be aware of pinch points to the hands and feet.
   11. Ensure all tubulars have securely installed protectors.
   12. Establish communications with the supply vessel.
   13. Attach the correct spreaders to the crane. Attach the tag lines as required.
   14. With the deck foreman in clear view of the Crane Operator, and a deck
     hand at each end of the pipe deck, hook onto the first bundle of tubulars.
   15. Before picking up the load, confirm that the correct slings have been
      picked up and that not more than one bundle is being picked up.
   16. Pick up the bundle and lower  it to the supply boat. Ensure personnel do
      not pass under suspended load.
   17. Position the bundle as per the directions of the supply boat deck crew and
      using the tag lines  if required land the bundle on the deck of the supply
       boat.
   18. Slack off sufficiently to prevent the whip line snatching with the movement
       of the vessels.
   19. Repeat the above until all the tubulars are back loaded.

Offloading from a Supply Vessel:

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               165


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 166 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                     Issue Date
                                                      Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                      January 2011
                     Revision No.
                                        Manual Nr:
                           0
                     Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                       Document Title:
                                                                 Procedures Manual
                    Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                    K.Tiersma                          Approved by


1. Obtain an up to date manifest of equipment to be loaded/offloaded from
   the storekeeper.
2. Establish communications with the supply vessel.
3. Attach the correct lifting spreaders to the crane and hook two taglines onto
   the spreaders.
4. Station one deck hand at each end of the pipe deck and the deck foreman
    in clear view of the crane operator.
5. Following boat crew instructions lower the spreaders to the deck of the
   boat allowing sufficient slack to prevent the whip-line from snatching due to
   the movement of the vessels.
6. Hook the spreaders onto the slings of the first bundle, pick it up following
   the instructions from the deck crew on the supply vessel, and bring the
   bundle inboard.
7. The deck foreman should be positioned in view of the crane and the two
   deck hands. If this is not possible, radios shall be used and he must stay in
   a position where he can see the deck crew.
8. Position the bundle over the pipe deck and position it using the tag lines.
   Never pass under a load and be aware of being trapped by a swinging
   load.
9. Land the load in the pipe bay close to the catwalk side with the box ends
   facing aft and approximately level with the aft end of the pipe deck.
10. Maintaining some tension on the crane, remove the bulldog grips and store
   them in a bucket.
11. Once the bull dog grips are removed the deck crew must move to the ends
    of the bundle.
12. Slack off on the crane ensuring the deck crew are well clear and allow the
   tubulars to roll out.
13. If the tubulars do not roll out use a pinch bar to assist. Be aware of the
   possibility that the tubulars can roll and trap feet, legs and hands.
14. Remove the transit slings and store them in an appropriate place to avoid
    trip hazards.
15. Repeat the above until the bay has a complete row laid out.
16. Place timber on top of each row when it is full before continuing to the next
17. It may be necessary to use casing hooks to dress the tubulars, if so care
   must be taken not to get hands and feet in pinch points.
18. If casing is being loaded remove the protectors and store in a dedicated
   skip or container.
19. Measure the casing as per the Operator Drilling Supervisor instructions.
   Use the appropriate deck tally sheets.
20. Paint the length of the joint at the box end of casing. Paint the number of
   the joint half of the way up the joint, starting from the outboard side.
21. Proceed to further bays as required and repeat until all the tubulars are
   onboard.
22. Baker-locked shoe track joints should be temporarily stored in other bays.


                Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               166


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 167 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   23. If drill pipe etc is being loaded leave the protectors on and dress the pipe in
         its bay with timber between each row.
   24. Tubulars may be transferred to the pipe hold if not immediately required in
      which case the bulldogs will be left on until the bundle is lowered into the
       hold.

Note: Sometimes  it may be desirable to offload the supply boat as soon as
possible. Therefore the casing may be laid in one pipe bay and left bundled until
later. After the boat has departed the tubulars will be laid out as above.

Post Task Actions:

    1. Store away any timber not in use.
    2. Store away or back load any transit slings.
    3. Store away any bulldog grips.
    4. Leave the worksite clean, tidy and hazard free.
    5. Return radios for charging if used.
    6. A correct tally of what has been taken onto the rig or backloaded should be
      given to the storekeeper.
    7. Review the procedure and JSA.





                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               167

 4.2.3.2 Drifting Casing and Tubulars

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to control the operation of drifting the casing and
tubing.

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried out prior to and during drifting the casing and tubing.

Responsible Persons:

Rig Superintendent   / Senior Toolpusher   /  Jr Toolpusher   /  Driller   / A/D   /
Derrickman

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

    1. Hold a pre-job meeting with all personnel involved. Review the procedure
     and JSA (Job Safety Analysis) for the task.
    2. Clear the work area of all slings, protectors, wood, etc.
    3. Attach one end of a length of rope about 2m longer than the casing to the
         drift and the other to an empty can, slightly smaller than the I.D. of the
       casing.
    4. An alternative is to use a length of ¾” ungalvanised wire 3m longer than
      the casing. Attach the drift with an eye splice or bulldog grip and push the
       wire through the casing instead of using compressed air.
    5. Attach another rope the same length to the back of the drift so that it can
     be pulled out of the joint if the drift will not pass through.
    6. P.P.E. must be worn.
    7.  Barrier off the area, hang signs and make a public announcement before
     commencing the task.
    8. Casing will have been laid out, strapped, protectors removed.
    9. Ensure the area at both ends of the casing is free from trip hazards, such
      as slings, wood and protectors.
   10. Check the drift is of the correct size for the casing and conforms to API
     Spec 5CT.
   11. Have some red paint and a brush to indentify any joints which will not drift.
     These joints must be clearly marked.

Operation:

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               168


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 169 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by


    1. Position a man at each end of the casing.
    2.  Starting at the first joint in the row, place the can into the box end of the
       casing. As long as the drift is made of a plastic type material, this can be
      the box or pin end.  If the drift is of a metal variety the casing must be
        drifted from the pin end only. This is to avoid damaging any seals on the
      box end.
    3. Point the nozzle of the airline into the end of the casing and turn on the air
       to blow the can to the pin end of the casing.
    4. Stand clear of the pin end during this operation, in case of any flying
       debris, but in a position to see the exiting can.
    5. When the can exits turn off the air.
    6. Place the can into the pin end of the next joint of casing and push the drift
       into box end of the first joint of casing. Pull the drift through the casing with
      the rope, until the drift is just inside the pin end of the joint.
    7. Blow the can through the casing to the box end in a repeat process of
      steps # 3 and # 4.
    8. Once the can has exited and the air has been turned off, lift the drift from
      the pin end of the first joint and place it into the pin of the next joint.
    9.  Pull the drift through to the box end of the joint.
   10. The process is now repeated until the drifting is complete.
   11. Although a can has been specified for attaching the rope to,  it is often
     more convenient  to use a rag or a  plastic  bag/bottle. This  is  quite
      acceptable.
   12. Depending on the height of the casing,  it may be necessary to use a
       platform or scaffolding to drift the higher rows.
   13. Pull the drift backwards out of any joints that will not drift and clearly mark
     them with red paint and note down the numbers.

Post Task Actions:

    1. Bleed off the airlines and stow away equipment. Check for any damage.
    2. Clean the drift and return to the company man's store.
    3. Clean and tidy work area.
    4. Remove barriers and make a public announcement.
    5. Close out any permits associated with this task.
    6. Review the procedure and JSA for any improvement opportunities.





                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               169


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 170 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by



 4.2.3.3 Use of the Stabbing Board

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to explain the process to be followed when lifting
personnel with air/hydraulic hoists.

Scope:

The procedure describes the entire process including the safety precautions to be
exercised and includes the equipment to be used when manriding.

Responsibility:

Rig Superintendent  / Senior-Toolpusher  / Jr Toolpusher  / Driller  / A/D  / Drilling
crew

Overview

The stabbing board is normally used for assisting the make up and latching of
elevators etc. while running casing. It can also be used for assisting in hanging off
the travelling assembly. Use of the stabbing board  is dangerous due to the
interface with the travelling assembly therefore  it must be used with stringent
rules regarding operation and use.

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

    1. Hold a pre job meeting confirming the hand signals to be used while using
      the stabbing board. Review the procedure and JSA (Job safety Analysis).
    2.  Prior to entering the derrick, inform the driller.
    3. Check the fall arrestor has current certification, is properly secured, and
       positioned correctly above the stabbing board. It should be secured to an
      anchorage independent of the stabbing board or frame.
    4. Wear a full body harness and before setting foot on the stabbing board pull
      the wire through the frame and  it clip on to the harness ensuring the
      carabineer is fully made up and tight.
    5. Carry out a visual inspection of the stabbing board basket and frame.
      Ensure all bolts and pins are secure. Also check the fastenings to the
       derrick and ensure they are tight.
    6. Check the grating and belly band is in good condition and secure.
    7. Check the air winch wire is properly spooled and in good condition. Check
      the steel lanyard and hoses on the winch control are in good condition.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               170


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 171 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

    8. Check the air supply hose is in good condition and secure with retaining
       pins and whip checks installed.
    9. Check the safety locks at the bottom of the stabbing board are engaged
     and the air winch wire is tight with the emergency locks disengaged.
   10. Ensure the camera is clean and has a good picture, as this will be used by
      the  Driller to monitor the position of the board and also to view the
       operator’s signals while latching the elevators.

Operation:

    1. Turn on the air supply.
    2.  Pull the handle to the  left of the stabbing board frame to release the
      mechanical latches.
    3. Hinge down the stabbing board (release the handle when the latches are
       clear) to its full extent using the left hand control on the stabbing board
      frame.
    4. When the stabbing board  is  fully lowered ensure  it does not foul the
       travelling block.
    5. Enter the stabbing board wearing a safety harness and fall arrestor.
    6. To raise or lower the stabbing board depress the foot pedal at the rear of
      the board and press the raise or lower levers on the pendant control.
      Adjust the position of the board to the required height and release the foot
      pedal to engage the safety locks.
    7. Before lowering the extension ensure the driller is informed.
    8. Lower the extension with the right hand control valve on the stabbing
      board frame.
    9.  After use raise the extension with the right hand control valve on the
      stabbing board frame.
   10. Inform the driller the extension is raised.
   11. Prior to lowering the blocks the Driller must check the camera to confirm
       that the stabbing board is clear of the travelling assembly and the drill crew
      should look up as another set of eyes.
   12. If waiting for further operations the board can be left in this position while
       there is someone in attendance (never leave the basket unattended while
      hinged down).
   13. If operations are continuing inform the driller each time the extension is
      hinged up or down.
   14. If no longer required depress the foot pedal and using the pendant control
       raise/lower the stabbing board to its parking position.
   15. Hinge the stabbing board up to its parked position with the left hand control
      handle on the frame ensuring the locks are engaged.
   16. Turn off the air supply.
   17. Remove and secure the fall arrestor wire.
   18. Recheck that all is secure and inform the driller that the stabbing board is
     unmanned and secure.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               171


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 172 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

Post Task Actions:

    1.  Visually inspect and function test the Stabbing board and record any faults.
    2. Clean and check harness for any damage and store in dedicated area.
    3. Review the procedure and the JSA.





                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               172

                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir

4.2.4  Drill Line


 4.2.4.1 Hanging Off the Travelling Block Assembly

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to control the operation of hanging off the
travelling blockassembly in the derrick for the following operations:

   •  Slip and cut drill line.
   •  Change out of drill line.
   •  Repairs or maintenance on CMC (Crown Mounted Compensator).
   •  Repairs or maintenance on drawworks.
   •  Repairs or maintenance of deadline anchor or load cell.

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried on and above the rig floor to hang off the travelling assembly.

Responsible persons:

Rig Superintendent / Senior Toolpusher /Jr Pusher / Driller / A/D / Drilling crew

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

    1.  If hanging off the travelling assembly during drilling operations carry out a
       flow check to ensure the well is stable then monitor the well on the trip
       tank.
    2. Always hang off the travelling assembly with the BHA above the BOP
      unless circumstances prevent it (damaged drill line etc).
    3. JSA, Pre-job Meeting and PTW (Permit to Work) (man riding) to be in
      place and discussed prior to commencing the job. Manriding procedures
      are covered in procedure 4.2.5.1 – Manriding Operations.
    4.  Lifting gear shall be properly inspected and with current colour coding.
    5. Proper PPE shall be worn for the appropriate operation area.
    6. Ensure good communication throughout the operation.
    7.  All moving equipment in the derrick must be locked off while men aloft.
    8. Senior Tool Pusher or Jr ToolPusher to be on rig-floor.



                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               173


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 174 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

    9. Before starting the operation, all necessary tools must have been checked
     and be in good working condition.
   10. Walkie-talkie UHF.

Hanging off the blocks:

    1.  If there is pipe in the rotary, install an open IBOP on the pipe in the rotary
     and cover it with a bucket. A cover should also be put around the slips to
      prevent items falling down the hole.
    2. Keep the  trip tank pump running and monitor the well throughout the
       operation.
    3. Raise the travelling assembly to a little above hang off height guided by the
       assistant driller from the derrick ladder as required.
    4. Switch off the drawworks and lock off the brake.
    5. A man will be taken up on the man-riding winch to the travelling block as
      per procedure 4.2.5.1 – Manriding Operations. The assistant driller will
       act as deck foreman communicating with all involved personnel.
    6.  If required a man can assist from the casing stabbing board.
    7. The drill crew will release one of the hang off lines and controlling it with a
      rope or hand winch allow it to swing towards well centre.
    8. The man in the riding belt will check the height of the travelling block and if
      too high will move away from the travelling block and drill line to a safe
       area.
    9. Adjust the height of the travelling block as per the instructions from the
     man in the riding belt and again lock off the brake.
   10. If the ship is moving excessively control the hang off line by tying  it off to
      the drill line temporarily.
   11. Shackle the hang off line to the shackle on the travelling block.
   12. Release the other hang off line and controlling it with a rope or hand winch
       allow it to swing towards well centre.
   13. Shackle the hang off line to the shackle on the travelling block ensuring
       that the retaining pins on all the shackles are in good condition and secure.
   14. The man in the riding belt will move away to a safe area and observe while
      the driller transfers the weight of the travelling assembly to the hang off
        lines.
   15. After ensuring that all is secure the man in the riding belt will come down
     and further operations can commence.





Picking up the blocks:




                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               174

    1. Once  the maintenance  or  repairs  are completed and  all  tools and
      personnel have been removed from the worksite the travelling assembly
      can be picked up again.
    2. Remove any permits or isolations.
    3. Switch on the drawworks and take the weight of the travelling assembly
       with the drawworks until the hang off lines are slack. Check the operation
       of the brakes etc.
    4. Lock off the brake and stop the drawworks.
    5. A man can then be taken up on the man-riding winch to the travelling block
      as procedure 4.2.5.1 – Manriding Operations. The assistant driller will act
      as deck foreman communicating with all involved personnel.
    6. Unshackle one of the hang off lines from the shackle on the travelling block
      ensuring that no parts are dropped. Make up the shackle again and install
      the retaining pin securely.
    7.  Pull back and secure this hang off line in the derrick ensuring  it will not
     come into contact with any moving equipment.
    8. Unshackle the other hang off line from the shackle on the travelling block
      ensuring that no parts are dropped. Make up the shackle again and install
      the retaining pin securely.
    9.  Pull back and secure this hang off line in the derrick ensuring  it will not
     come into contact with any moving equipment.
   10. After a final check of the travelling block conclude man-riding operations
     and sign off any PTW.
   11. Remove the covers and IBOP from the pipe and slips in the rotary  (if
       installed) and continue normal operations.

Post Task Actions:

    1. Check hang off lines securely tied back and clear of top drive when.
    2. Check hang of shackles tight with retaining pins in place.
    3. Clear away and inspect riding belts etc.

 4.2.4.2 Slipping and Cutting the Drilling Line

Maintain a record of the ton-miles on the drill line. The drill line will constantly be
inspected for various types of damage.  Refer to IADC Drilling Manual, 11th
Edition, 1992, Chapter M, "Wire Lines," along with manufacture’s guides for
inspection guidance and criteria.  Consider the following items for slipping and
cutting of drill line:

   •  Bottom Hole Assembly above the BOPs
   •   Install safety valve with valve open.
   •  Flow check.
   •  Monitor well on trip tank while slipping and cutting drill line.
   •  Mark number of wraps to be slipped and wraps to be cut.
   •  Cut the wire with cable cutter.
   •  Secure the fast line after cutting the drill line.

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to control the operation of slipping and cutting
the drill line.

Scope:

The following procedure describes the required  activities, which need to be
carried on the rig floor, prior to and during the slip and cut operation.

Responsible persons:

Rig Superintendent  / Senior Toolpusher /Jr Toolpusher  / Driller  / A/D  / Drilling
crew

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

    1. JSA, Pre-job Meeting and PTW (man riding and hot work) to be in place
     and discussed prior to commencing the job.
    2.  Lifting gear shall be properly inspected.
    3. Safety rule: Slip and cut drill line with the BHA above the BOP or out of the
      hole (compulsory).
    4. The wire is to be handled hand over hand and not slipped through the
     hands to prevent snagging and hand injuries.
    5. Proper PPE shall be worn for the appropriate operation area.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               176


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 177 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

    6. Good communication.
    7. Sr Toolpusher or Jr Toolpusher to be on rig-floor.

Preparation of Equipment:

    1. Before starting the operation, all necessary tools must have been checked
     and be in good working condition.
    2. Brass sledge hammer if available.
    3. Two impact wrenches, w/sockets size: 1⅜” - 1½”.
    4. Hammer wrench size; 1⅜” – 1½”.
    5. Wrench: 1⅜” – 1½”.
    6. 8” Adjustable wrench.
    7.  Drill line hydraulic cutter.
    8. Safety slings 5 ton, 2 meters long and specific 1 ⅝” wire clamp.
    9. 3 walkie-talkies UHF.
   10. Piece of 4” x 3” timber and 2 slings 5 tons with adequate shackles.
   11. Drawworks lighting tested and correctly installed.
   12. White paint and brush.
   13. Calliper for OD drill line check.

Operations:

    1. Before commencing ensure the well is monitored on the trip tank and is
       stable.
    2. The  Driller  is in charge of the operation, from the Drillers cabin, and
      supervised by the TP.
    3. Ensure good communication throughout.
    4.  Drill Line to be visually inspected for any damage to be taken in account
       while slipping and cutting (length to be slipped & cut).
    5. Rig floor to be barriered off to all un-necessary personnel.
    6. Set the string in the slips, unlatch the elevators and install an IBOP valve in
      the open position.
    7. Stroke the CMC up and lock it in open position.
    8. Hang off the travelling assembly as per procedure 4.2.4.1 – Hanging Off
      the Travelling Assembly, ensuring the weight indicator reads zero.

Slipping the Drill Line:

    1. The Driller is to select Slip and Cut mode in DCR.
    2. Mark the drawworks drum measuring from the wire the same number of
      wraps that are to be cut off (10’or 3m per wrap).
    3. The next operation is at the Deadline anchor in the Derrick.
    4. Remove the drill line safety clamp, loosen the deadline clamp bolts and
     remove the clamping plates. Then remove the retaining bolts and locking
       pins from the deadline anchor.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               177


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 178 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

    5. Check that the drill line drum cover is removed and the drum free to turn
       with the spooler.
    6. When ready, slowly spool on the drilling line monitoring the weight (do not
      exceed the block weight) and ensuring that the line runs freely on the dead
     man anchor.
    7. Stop when the wire reaches the mark on the drawworks drum, and then
      ensure that sufficient wire is slipped to bring the weight to back to zero.
    8. Once the desired length is slipped, re-instate the locking pins and retaining
        bolts. Reinsert the clamping plates and tighten the bolts (150kn or 670nm)
     on the anchor clamps and reinstall the safety clamp.
    9. Confirm that the deadline anchor clamp is OK. Paint a white line across the
     3 wires on the deadline anchor.

Cutting the Drill Line:

    1. As per the length to be cut (10ft or 3m per wrap) count the number of
      wraps on the drawworks drum. Count from the dead man and paint a white
     mark on the wire.
    2. Secure the fast line with a 1⅝” clamp (or rope/chain etc) and sling to an
      anchorage in front of the drawworks.
    3. Switch the drawworks into reverse and slowly spool the drill line off the
     drum until the paint mark is on the drill floor.
    4. Tape the wire where  it is to be cut, and then cut the wire  it using a
       hydraulic cutter.
    5. Continue to spool the wire off the drum aided by the drill crew. The wire is
       to be handled hand over hand to prevent hand injuries coiling it on the drill
        floor with a sling in position for lifting it later.
    6. When the end of the drum is reached, the deadman will be positioned for
      access by the drill crew.
    7. Switch off the Drawworks motors and confirm this.
    8. Push the wire through the drum to remove the dead man from its location
     on the drum.
    9. Remove the dead man and pull the wire off the drum, then coil and sling it
        for disposal by the crane.
   10. Carry out a visual inspection and an OD measurement on the fast line,
      then push   it through the drum,  install the dead man  in the correct
       orientation and torque the bolts to the correct value.
   11. Locate the dead-man in it’s retainer on the drum and snug the wire into the
     Lebus groove on the drum, using a brass hammer if available.
   12. Remove all tools from inside the DW drum. Clear personnel from the area.
   13. Switch on the drawworks, select “hoist” and with the drill crew tensioning
      the wire slowly spool on the slack drilling line until all slack is taken up.
   14. Remove the fast line clamp.
   15. Carry out a visual inspection of the brake bands, pads, linkages and
       equalizer bar and make any adjustments or repairs required.
   16. Select tripping/drilling in DCR.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               178

   17. Take the weight of the Blocks until the hang off line is slack.
   18. Remove the hang off line as per procedure 4.2.4.1 – Hanging Off the
      Travelling Assembly.
   19. Reset and test the crown 'o’ Matic. Record in the IADC report.
   20. Remove the IBOP from the joint in the rotary and latch the elevators onto
      the string. Take weight without pulling the slips and visually check the
      deadline anchor and drawworks drum.
   21. If all is secure set the slips, unlatch the elevators and slowly raise the
      blocks stopping every 20m of drill line (6 wraps of the drum) to make a
       visual inspection of the wire and to measure the OD.
   22. Record the results and inform the Toolpusher of any excessive wear or
      broken strands. Record the slip and cut in the drill line log and IADC report.
   23. Stroke down the CMC while continuing to hoist and inspect the drill line.
   24. Reset the Block Calibration in Drillview System.
   25. Continue to RIH (open CMC to enter BOP).

Post task actions:

    1. Record any static losses which may have occurred while   monitoring the
       well during the operation.
    2. Remove the old drill line from the drill floor.
    3. Clear away all tools used.
    4. Handover to the oncoming Driller that Slip and Cut has been completed.
    5. Record slip and cut on IADC report and in the Ton mile records.
    6. Review the procedure and JSA.

 4.2.4.3 Change out the Drilling Line

Purpose:

The purpose of this procedure is to list all equipment and steps required to
perform this job efficiently and safely.

Scope:

The procedure describes the entire process including the safety precautions to be
exercised and includes the equipment to be used when changing a drilling line.

Responsible Persons:

Rig Superintendent / Tool Pusher / Driller / First Officer

Procedure:

Safety Precautions and Preparation:

Preparation to be carried out at least 24 hours before planned job with Hot Work
Permit.

    1. Pre-job meeting  to be held with the Tool Pusher and  all personnel
       involved. Review the JSA.
    2. Designate: 3 persons on reel, 2 persons on dead line anchor, 2 persons on
      the drawworks. 1 person at the crown. 1 person to observe the travelling
       block. Each group must have a radio.
    3. Close drill floor area with barrier tape and post warning signs “OVERHEAD
     DANGER”.
    4. Make a P/A Announcement.
    5. Spool off 30m and cut the wire, remove the spooler drive chain and tear
     down the old reel from its support.
    6.  Install the new drum on its stand with the drill line coming off the bottom of
      the drum on the inboard side, uncoil 20m of wire.
    7. Splice the new drilling line to the old deadline.
    8. Welder to make the splice assisted as required and supervised by the Tool
      Pusher.
    9. On each of the wires to be spliced measure 11m and mark the strands.
      Untwist the strands slightly beyond the marks. Cut off the alternate strands
     and the core with a hydraulic wire cutter.
   10. Clean the cut ends with thinner to take off all grease.
   11. Splice the two wires together and braze the joints.


                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               180


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 181 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

   12. Paint the full length of the splice with white paint highlighting the brazed
        joints.
   13. Re-instate drill line spooler motor drive chain.

Equipment Required:

   •  Two air operated wrenches & air hoses, with sockets size: 36-38 mm and
      spanners and impact wrench together with large hammer.
   •  Hydraulic wire cutter tested and in good working condition.
   •  Safety sling 5 Tons, 2m long and the safety 1 ¾ clamp.
   •  1 5/8” bulldog clamp.
   •  New bushing set for 1 5/8” wire line, to change out at dead anchor clamp if
      needed.
   •  2 Chain loops or bulldog grips (to hold the drill line).
   •  2 pieces of wood to use as a brake band on reel (as the motor must not be
      connected to the reel).
   •  4 radios.
   •  Barrier tape.
   •  Drawworks lighting tested and in place.
   •  White paint and brush.
   •  8” Adjustable wrench.

Operation:

    1. Reeving the Drill Line.
    2. Leave the CMC in the closed position (to be able to check the crown block
      sheaves).
    3. Hang the blocks  off, as per procedure  4.2.4.1 – Hanging Off the
      Travelling Assembly, ensuring the weight indicator reads zero.
    4. Remove the safety clamp from the  drill  line adjacent to the deadline
      anchor.
    5. Loosen the deadline anchor clamp and remove the clamping  plates.
     Remove the retaining bolts and locking pins from the deadline anchor.
    6. Note how many layers and wraps are on the drawworks drum prior to
     commencing the operation.
    7. Commence slipping the new wire into the system spooling the wire onto
      the drawworks drum. The crew to inform the Driller by radio as the wire
       splice passes through the following critical points ensuring that the splice
       doesn’t snag the jumper bars and rope guards:
          •  Past the guide bracket on the drill floor
          •  Dead line anchor idler sheave
          •  Dead line anchor
          •  Starboard CMC idler sheave
          •  Crown cluster sheaves
          •  Travelling block sheaves

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               181


────────────────────────────────────────────
[ صفحه 182 از 286 ]
────────────────────────────────────────────
                         Issue Date
                                                          Unit:                     Iran Amir Kabir
                          January 2011
                         Revision No.
                                            Manual Nr:
                               0
                         Revision Date                                             Drilling Operations
                                          Document Title:
                                                                     Procedures Manual
                       Prepared by                        Reviewed and
                        K.Tiersma                          Approved by

          •  Port side CMC idler sheave
          •  Fast line roller guide (if fitted).
    8. When the splice reaches the drawworks, spool 3 wraps of the new line
      onto the drum.
    9. Secure the fast line with a clamp chain or rope. Select reverse and start
      unspooling the wire from the drawworks drum, assisted by the drill crew,
        until the splice is reached.
   10. Cut off the old line including the splice with the hydraulic cutter.

Unspooling the old line to the catwalk:


    1. Lay out timber on the catwalk and have a 5m long 10tonne sling ready.
    2. Have the deck crew (8 persons approx) standing by to lower the drill line to
      the catwalk.
    3.  Install a 1 5/8” lazy sheave with 3m sling on the drill floor pad eye in front
       of the bottom racker cabin, and pass the drilling line through  it prior to
      passing the end of the line down the V door ramp .
    4. Unspool the drilling line from the drawworks flaking the wire out on the
      catwalk until 3 wraps are left on the drum.
    5. Secure the drill line on the drill floor with a chain and bull dog clamp (1
         5/8”).
    6. Continue to un-spool the drill line off the drum positioning the dead man
      clamp for access by the  drill crew. Push the wire through the drum to
     remove the dead man clamp. Loosen the bolts and remove the clamp from
      the drill line.
    7.  Pull the wire off the drum and out of the lazy sheave.
    8.  Install a 1 5/8” safety clamp and sling on the wire and take the weight of the
          drill line with the V door air winch.
    9. Remove the retaining clamp and sling from the drill line at the top of the V
      door ramp and lower the end of the drill line to the catwalk with the air
      winch.
   10. Feed the new line onto the drawworks drum and install the dead man
      clamp, set at the correct orientation and torqued to the desired value (A jig
       to hold the clamp while it is torqued up is particularly beneficial). Remove
         all tools used from the drawworks area.
   11. Ensure the clamp is properly located and snug the wire into the Lebus
      groove.
   12. Tension and guide the wire (hand over hand) until the slack is taken up,
      then continue to spool the new line onto the drawworks drum, observing
      the wire at the critical points as it slips through the system.
   13. Spool on the same number  of  layers and wraps as noted  prior  to
     commencing the operation. Ensure the weight indicator reads zero.
   14. Reinstate the locking pins and retaining bolts on the dead line anchor and
      secure the clamp and safety clamp, and then torque the bolts to the
      desired torque. Mark the wires on the deadline anchor drum.

                   Iran Amir Kabir Rig Specific Drilling Procedures               182

[ صفحه 183 از 286 ]

                         Issue Date

   15. Confirm to the driller that the deadline anchor is secure.
   16. Take up any slack wire with the drill line spooler and re-cover the drum.
   17. Reinstall the guide at the drill floor.
   18. Carry out a visual inspection on the brake pads / rims etc and check the
       equalising bar is correctly spaced. Check the gap on the Load Sensor
       (5/8") and adjust if necessary.
   19. Check the kickback rollers for correct operation and gap size.
   20. Replace the Crown ‘o’ Matic toggle and check for correct operation. This
         will be checked and reset.
   21. Replace the Drawworks front cover. Watch for ‘pinch points’ between the
      cover and the Drawworks.
   22. Pick up the weight of the travelling assembly checking the drawworks
      drum, deadline clamp and the deadline anchor.
   23. Remove the block hang off lines as per procedure 4.2.4.1 – Hanging Off
      the Travelling Assembly. Ensure they are properly secured and well out
       of the way.
   24. Re-set the Crown ‘o’ Matic and MMI.
   25. Rig down all equipment used during the operation.

Post Task Actions:

    1.  Fully open and lock the CMC. Slowly lower the travelling assembly to the
          drill  floor checking  that the correct number  of wraps remain on the
      drawworks drum.
    2. Close the CMC while raising the travelling assembly to the crown to test
      the safety crown savers and checking good spooling on drawworks drum.
    3. Repeat this twice at moderate and  full speed, checking the drawworks
       spooling. Recheck the dead man and deadline anchor clamps are secure
      before commencing operations.
    4. Review the procedure and JSA.
